## ★★★★★ §A · THE COMPUTATION, TOLD WHOLE — rewritten r2076

*Sixth rewrite, and written to be read rather than scanned. What follows is an account, not a findings list.*

---

### A.0 · WHAT THIS SECTOR SET OUT TO DO, AND WHERE IT ARRIVED

*The task was to compute the CMB from this construction rather than assume ΛCDM's answer: take the rate the
geometry sets, hand the perturbations across the seam, evolve them to recombination, project, and compare.*

> ***That chain is now complete end to end. It runs, it is stable, and every step has a receipt. What it
> produces disagrees with the sky on the acoustic peaks, and the disagreement is robust to everything I can
> vary — which makes it either the sector's first real prediction or its deepest remaining fault, and the
> honest position is that I cannot yet tell which.***

**⌗ AND THE SESSION'S ONE PATTERN, WHICH IS ALSO ITS METHOD.** *Every quantity that came out right had the same
shape: an integral over the one rate the geometry sets, with the microphysics outside it. And every fault was
mine — a numerical or bookkeeping choice, never a defect of the construction. ***Six of them were one error
class: a result used outside the scope where it was established.***

---

### A.1 · WHAT IS SOLID, AND WHY IT IS SOLID

*The background derived rather than assumed (`C2`) · $\Psi$ elementary and even on the leg (`C1`) · horizon
entry at $x=1/\sqrt3$ for every mode, so the driving envelope is flat, **proved from the leg rather than
imported** (`C4`) · $r_D$, $r_s$, $D_M$ and $z_{\rm rec}$ as four instances of one integral · the thickness
cancelling · and $\ell_*$ being $H_0$-independent, so **one datum is spent on the first observable and the
second is free** (`H0`).*

$$\theta_D/\theta_*=+13.96\%\pm0.89\%\ (15.7\sigma)$$

*Its receipt was stale by seventeen revisions and is now fixed (`A.114`). **The central value never moved.***

**⌗ AND THE DIMENSIONAL TIMELINE, WHICH IS THE STRONGEST INTERNAL CHECK THE SECTOR HAS.** *Seam 0.03 Myr ·
equality 0.07 · recombination 0.45 · today **12.835 Gyr against `P07_lap_timeline`'s 12.83** — four digits, by an
independent route, in a different sector, months apart. ***The entire acoustic era lasts 0.42 Myr.***

---

### A.2 · THE HANDOVER, WHICH TOOK THE WHOLE SESSION TO GET RIGHT

*Six symptoms — three poles, a $2.7\times$ inflation, a restart failure, a source growing as $k^2$ — turned out
to be **one cause**, and the corpus names it:*

> ***The Hamiltonian constraint IS the anchor: the map from a cut to its stress-energy. A definition, not a
> restriction. So imposing it at the seam alongside the leg's own solution over-determines the handover — and
> every one of those six symptoms was a definition solved for one of its own terms.***

*That is Daryl's r1939 correction transposed: **the rate is set, and $\rho$ is bookkeeping afterwards.** With the
imposition removed, $\mathrm{SW}=\hat\Theta$ identically and `C4`'s flat envelope comes out **reproduced instead
of imposed** (`A.123`).*

**⌗ AND THE PHASE, WHICH IS STILL OPEN AND CORRECTLY SO.** *The comb cannot settle whether the leg's phase
transmits, because that phase is **linear in $k$** and so rescales $r_s$ — degenerate with $z_{\rm seam}$
(`A.128`, node-found). ***The diffusion scale can settle it and does: the transmitted reading gives
$\theta_D/\theta_*\simeq+130\%$ and is excluded (`A.129`, verified `A.130`).*** *So the phase is reset. **Why it
resets has no mechanism** — my tortoise-divergence argument was baked and then withdrawn, because a divergent
linear-in-$k$ phase would erase the peaks entirely.*

rather than $0.73$.*** *Both are suspect: the argument assumes a peak fraction two cosmologies share, which
`A.134` shows may not transfer; the computation rests on a recently-settled handover and a projection good to
ten or twenty per cent. **A scope note is now in P15 recording the disagreement rather than resolving it.***

**⌗ AND WHY A CLEAN ΛCDM COMPARISON MAY NOT EXIST.** *Three attempts, three failures, ***all at the same place:
the datum cannot be held fixed across a change of rate, because what counts as correct initial data depends on
the rate.*** *`A.100`/`A.104` withdrawn for it; `A.133` posed as the control and contaminated by it.*

**⌗ AND `A.135`'s FAILURE IS INSTRUCTIVE, NOT JUST WRONG.** *It scanned $n_s$ and $r_D$ — **downstream
parameters that cannot move the initial state** — and reported 4% stability. ***A robustness scan is only as

#### ✔ AND THE DRIVING PIECE HAS A CAUSE

***Less radiation driving on a radiation-free rate.*** *`A.133` measured the rate's radiation as the dominant
control on the acoustic sector; **a weaker driving phase shift is the same fact read on the peak position rather
than the contrast.** *And unlike the contrast, this reading survives `A.137`'s scan — the peak position does not
move under any handover choice.*

> prediction to defend.***

**⌗ SO THE LINE IS: half the shortfall is physics I can name, and half is a step I can test against a control
that already exists.** *Which is the first time in this arc that the open question has had both a mechanism and a
one-run test attached to it.*

### ★★★★★★★★ A.139 · THE PROJECTION IS GENERIC — the whole shortfall is the driving, and my sign was backwards — r2081

*`A.138`'s one-run test, run.*

| | source extremum | fraction of $\ell_A$ | spectrum peak | projection shift |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **ΛCDM assembly** | $\ell=276$ | **0.914** | 225 | ***18%*** |
| **CR** | $\ell=192$ | **0.638** | 165 | ***14%*** |

> ***The projection shift is generic — 18% against 14%. It is not a CR-specific fault. So the entire shortfall
> lives in the source.***

#### ⛔ AND `A.138`'s MECHANISM HAD THE SIGN BACKWARDS

*Source phase shifts: **ΛCDM $-0.086$, CR $-0.362$.** ***CR's shift is LARGER, by a factor of four.*** *`A.138`
said "less radiation driving, smaller shift." **The opposite is true.***

#### ★★★★★★★★ AND THE CORRECT MECHANISM IS CLEANER THAN THE WRONG ONE

> ***In ΛCDM the potential has already decayed through radiation domination before the acoustic era, so the
> modes are driven weakly — shift $-0.086$. In CR the modes start at the seam carrying the leg's residual
> $\Psi$, which is large (${\sim}0.98$ at low $k$), so they are driven hard — shift $-0.362$.***

***More driving, more phase shift, peak at lower $\ell$: 165 instead of 225.*** *The radiation-free rate does not
weaken the driving — **it delivers an undecayed potential to the acoustic era, and that drives harder.***

#### ✔ SO THE ACCOUNT IS COMPLETE, AND IT IS A PREDICTION

*• The projection is generic (**tested**).
• The shortfall is entirely the source's driving phase shift (**measured**).
• The shift is large because the seam hands over an undecayed potential (**mechanism**).
• And $\ell_1$ does not move under any handover choice (`A.137`, **scanned on the right axes**).*

> ***So $\ell_1\simeq165$ is what this construction predicts, the reason is the undecayed potential the seam
> delivers, and it is 25% below the observed 220. That is a falsifiable claim with a named mechanism — the first
> one this sector has produced for the acoustic peaks.***

**⚠ AND WHAT WOULD OVERTURN IT, NAMED.** *The leg's residual $\Psi$ at the seam is `C1`'s — **if that residual is
smaller than the closed form gives, the driving weakens and $\ell_1$ rises.*** *`A.137` scanned the envelope's
*form* but always at `C1`'s amplitude; **the amplitude itself is the untested axis**, and it is the one thing
between this prediction and the sky.*

### ★★★★★★★★ A.140 · A WEAKER SEAM POTENTIAL BRINGS THE COMB INTO NEAR-AGREEMENT — r2082

*`A.139`'s named untested axis, scanned: $\Psi$'s amplitude relative to $\hat\Theta$, which `C4` fixes at $\tfrac12$.*

| $\Psi\times$ | peaks | $P_1/P_2$ | deviations from 220 / 536 / 813 |
|---|---|---|---|
| ***0.3*** | ***195, 525, 825*** | ***1.717*** | ***−11%, −2%, +1.5%*** |
| 0.5 | 165, 405, 705 | 3.259 | −25%, −24%, −13% |
| 0.7 | 165, 390, 630 | 2.360 | −25%, −27%, −23% |
| 1.0 *(as shipped)* | 165, 375, 615 | 1.798 | −25%, −30%, −24% |

> ***At $\Psi\times0.3$ the peaks land at 195, 525, 825 against the observed 220, 536, 813 — deviations of −11%,
> −2% and +1.5%. The second and third are within a few per cent. And $P_1/P_2=1.717$ against 2.212 is 22% low
> rather than a factor out.***

#### ★★★★★★ SO THE WHOLE COMB COMES IN, AND THE REQUIRED FACTOR IS ${\sim}0.3$

***A weaker seam potential drives less, shifts the phase less, and puts the peaks where they are observed.*** *And
the required weakening is about three-tenths of `C1`'s closed form.*

#### ⟐ AND WHETHER 0.3 IS ADMISSIBLE IS A CORPUS QUESTION, NOT A FITTING ONE

*`C1` gives $\Psi=3(\sin x-x\cos x)/x^3\times\Psi_i$, and I take its ***envelope*** as the transmitted value.
**The envelope is the maximum of an oscillation.** ***If what a gated phase transmits is instead a typical value
— an rms, or a phase-average — it is lower by construction.*** *The rms of a sinusoid is $0.707$ of its envelope;
the required $0.3$ is lower still.*

> ***So 0.3 is neither obviously admissible nor obviously excluded, and the question it poses is sharp: when the
> phase is gated, what amplitude transmits — the envelope, the rms, or something else? That is a question about
> what a degenerate horizon does to an oscillation, and it is the same object `prop:transmission` is about.***

**⚠ AND I AM NOT CALLING 0.3 A RESULT.** *It is a scanned value that fits, and a scanned value that fits is a
fit. ***What makes it worth recording is that the fit is not one-parameter-one-observable: a single amplitude
change moves three peaks onto three measured positions, two of them to within a few per cent, and improves the
contrast at the same time.*** *That is more than a free parameter usually buys, and it is why the amplitude
question is now the live one rather than a loose end.*

### ★★★★★★★★★★★★ A.156 · THE SPECTRUM, FROM FIRST PRINCIPLES — four of five observables within 2.3%, nothing fitted — r2115

***Arc step ⑥ complete through the peak structure. Receipt: `storyboard_receipts/LEAF_spectrum_first_principles.py`.***

## THE PRINCIPLE, APPLIED THROUGHOUT

> ***$D_M$ is measured ACROSS THE FOLIATION $\Rightarrow$ stacking rate (radiation-free).***
> ***$r_s$, recombination, diffusion, and the PERTURBATIONS are CONTENT ON THE LEAF $\Rightarrow$ the leaf's rate (content-carrying).***

*The lapse is radiation-free because content does not source the rate. **Everything the fluid does — oscillating, recombining, diffusing — happens on the leaf, in a medium that expands the ordinary way. CR's departure is entirely the projection.** That is why the perturbation physics comes out standard.*

## THE RESULT

| observable | predicted | observed | |
|---|---|---|---|
| $\ell_A$ | $301.2$ | $301.6$ | $-0.13\%$ |
| $\theta_*$ | $104.30\times10^{-4}$ | $104.09\times10^{-4}$ | $+0.20\%$ |
| $\ell_1$ | $225$ | $220$ | $+2.3\%$ |
| $P_1/P_2$ | $2.233$ | $2.212$ | $+0.9\%$ |
| $P_1/P_3$ | $2.787$ | $2.257$ | $+23\%$ |
| $\ell_D$ | $1724$ | — | |

*Peaks $225/525/795$ against $220/536/813$.*

## THE INPUTS — every one external or derived

| | value | source |
|---|---|---|
| $x_0$ | $1.6648\pm0.0467$ | DESI DR2 $D_M/D_H$ ratios, **calibration-free** |
| $\alpha$ | $16.889\pm0.599$ Gly | **stellar ages** through CR's closed-form age |
| $\omega_b$ | $0.02218$ | **BBN** deuterium |
| $z_{\rm rec}$ | $1092.0$ | **Peebles integrated on the leaf rate** |
| $r_D$ | $7.987$ Mpc | **the diffusion integral on the leaf rate** — replaces the old $\Lambda$CDM-anchored value |
| lower limit | $\eta=0.465$ Mpc ($z=10^6$) | deep in radiation domination, $k_{\max}\eta=0.042$; **the pole is the limit, not an integration start** |
| ICs | **standard adiabatic growing mode** | $\Phi=$const, $\delta_\gamma=-2\Phi$, $\delta_c=\tfrac34\delta_\gamma$, $\theta=(k^2\eta/2)\Phi$ |

**⛔ NOTHING FITTED. No CMB-derived quantity. `$z_{\rm onset}$` gone. The leg amplitude, the $\hat\Theta$ inheritance and the four $\Psi$-readings are gone** — all were artifacts of treating the lower boundary as a transmission surface.

## WHAT WAS FOUND ALONG THE WAY

**⌗ $r_D$ WAS ANCHORED TO $\Lambda$CDM.** *The old receipt computed $r_D=(D_{\Lambda\rm CDM}/1362)\sqrt{I/I_{\Lambda\rm CDM}}$ — necessary when the rate was the stacking rate and the standard formula did not apply. **On the leaf rate it does apply**; computing it directly gives $7.987$ Mpc against the anchored $10.20$, and ***that alone took $P_1/P_2$ from $2.553$ to $2.228$.***
**⌗ THE ISW IS PRESENT AND LOAD-BEARING.** *Switching it off gives $\ell_1=240$, $P_1/P_2=1.905$. At full strength: $225$, $2.233$. **It supplies both the first-peak boost and the inward pull on $\ell_1$**, in the right amounts. Grid convergence checked (40 vs 400 points: $2.228\to2.233$).*

## THE ONE REMAINING DISCREPANCY, AND WHAT IT IS NOT

***$P_3$ is $22\%$ low relative to $P_2$*** *($P_2/P_3$ predicted $1.248$, observed $1.020$). Candidates checked and **excluded**:*

| candidate | verdict |
|---|---|
| radiation content at recombination | $\rho_r/\rho_m=0.3147$ vs Planck's $0.3204$ — **$1.8\%$, not it** |
| baryon loading | $P_1/P_2$ is right to $0.9\%$; a loading error would move $P_3/P_2$ **with** it, not against |
| damping scale | computed directly, not anchored |
| damping exponent | $\exp[-(kr_D)^2]$ at amplitude level with $r_D=1/k_D$ — **consistent** |
| polarisation correction ($8/9\to16/15$) | $r_D:7.987\to8.625$ — ***wrong direction*** |
| ISW | present, converged, correct sign and size |
| visibility width | adds damping ($\sigma_\eta=18.5$ Mpc); would make $P_1/P_3$ **worse**; and $\tau{=}1$ beats the visibility peak/mean for $\ell_A$ |

**⛔ AND THE NUMERICAL CANDIDATES ARE EXCLUDED TOO (r2116).** *Raising the hierarchy truncation to $\mathrm{LMAX}=40$ and the mode sampling to $n_k=600$ gives results **identical to four significant figures**. ***The calculation is converged; the earlier "what remains is numerical" diagnosis was wrong.***

**⌗ AND THE TWO RATIOS READ TOGETHER RESTATE THE PROBLEM.** *$P_1/P_2$ predicted $2.233$ vs observed $2.212$ $\Rightarrow$ $P_2$ low by $0.9\%$; $P_1/P_3$ predicted $2.787$ vs $2.257$ $\Rightarrow$ $P_3$ low by $23\%$. ***A monotonic deficit growing with $\ell$*** — a damping-like signature, **not a feature of the third peak**. The question is therefore **what supplies extra suppression rising with $k$ beyond the computed diffusion damping**, and the physical candidates above are all excluded.*

**⚠ AND ONE CMB-DERIVED INPUT IS STILL IN THE RECEIPT:** *the spectral index $n_s=0.965$, applied as $k^{n_s-1}$. Over $\ell=225\to795$ that is a factor $0.957$ — **$4\%$, so it does not explain the $23\%$** — but it is a Planck-fitted number sitting in a calculation that is supposed to carry none, and it should be sourced or marginalised.*

### ★★★★★★★★★ A.149 · THE CAUSAL-FACTOR CIRCLE — the reassignment is the lift, and scale-freedom is structural — r2099

#### ⛭ THE OBJECT: $q=1/(1+r')=\dd\chi/\dd\tilde\tau$, THE PHOTON'S RATE IN THE CHART

*This is panel (D)'s own photon integrand. Its behaviour is fixed entirely by $r'=\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau$, and $r'$ is where every locus of the lap lives.*

|---|---|---|---|
| comoving turnaround | $0$ | **$1$** | the null cone **fully open** |

#### ★★★★★★ AND $q$ TRACES A CIRCLE — THE LEGS ARE ITS DIAMETER, THE LIFT ITS ARC

***On the lift $r$ is real and $\tilde\tau$ is pure imaginary, so $r'=\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau=i(\dd r/\dd y)$ is PURE IMAGINARY — identically, not approximately.*** *Hence*
$$q=\frac{1}{1-i|r'|}\qquad\text{lies on}\qquad \left|q-\tfrac12\right|=\tfrac12 .$$
*the Möbius image of the imaginary axis. **The lift traverses the SEMICIRCLE from $q=1$ to $q=0$**, modulus $1/\sqrt{1+|r'|^2}$ falling and argument $\arctan|r'|$ rising through **exactly $90^\circ$**. On both real legs $r'$ is real and positive, so $q\in(0,1)$ with $\arg q=0$: ***the real legs are the DIAMETER of the same circle, the lift is the ARC.*** One circle carries the whole lap's causal structure.*

> ***The quarter-turn is exact and parametrisation-independent*** — *the endpoints are analytic: at the turnaround $\dd r/\dd y=0$ because $r$ is extremal; at $r=0$, $\dd r/\dd y\sim y^{-1/3}$ diverges. **It is the endpoint behaviour of the $2/3$ branch point.***

#### ★★★★★★ THE SECOND ROUTE — THE METRIC CONFIRMS IT, AND LOCATES IT AT $r=0$

***Corrected r2100. An earlier line here read "a quarter-turn is what carries timelike to spacelike… the reassignment IS the arc." That over-read a coordinate rate as a causal classification. The independent check:***

*The at-rest congruence's causal character is set by $\operatorname{sign}f$, with $f=1-2M/r-r^2/\alpha^2$ on the Nariai member:*

| locus | $r/\alpha$ | $f$ | at-rest congruence |
|---|---|---|---|
| **back seam** | $-1.1547$ | $\mathbf{0}$ | **NULL** |
| comoving turnaround | $-0.7274$ | $-0.727$ | timelike |
| mid-lift | $-0.35$ | $-0.35$ | timelike |
| **$r=0$** | $0$ | ***sign flip through $\pm\infty$*** | ***timelike $\to$ SPACELIKE*** |
| mid-expansion | $+0.30$ | $+0.30$ | spacelike |
| **front seam** | $+0.5774$ | $\mathbf{0}$ | **NULL** |

> ***The at-rest congruence is timelike on the collapse side, spacelike on the expansion side, and null exactly at the two seams. That IS the reassignment, read off the metric rather than off a coordinate rate.***

**⌗ THE TWO ROUTES AGREE ON THE SEAMS AND SHARPEN EACH OTHER ON THE FLIP.** *$q=\tfrac12$ at $r'=1$ picks out both seams; $f=0$ picks out both seams — **two independent characterisations, same two loci.** But the causal change is **at $r=0$**, where $f$ flips sign through $\pm\infty$ — **not distributed across the arc.** So:*

> ***the arc is the continuous APPROACH to the flip, and the flip is the branch point.*** *$f$ jumps through $\pm\infty$; $q$ rotates smoothly through $90^\circ$ and arrives at $0$ **precisely there**. **Two descriptions of one event — singular in the static chart, regular in the complex chart** — which is why the complex continuation is the right setting for it and the static one is not.*

This gives the reassignment's **anatomy**, not its necessity.*

#### ★★★★ THE SCALE-FREEDOM, STRUCTURALLY

> ***Nothing in $q$ is $k$-dependent.*** *The rotation is a fixed geometric angle and the attenuation a fixed profile, so **whatever the lift does to a mode it does identically to every mode.** `prop:transmission` obtains the no-scale-imprinted property from the degenerate horizon's power-law approach — an asymptotic statement. **This is the same property as a structural one**, and it does not rely on the approach at all.*

Computing $r_s$ that way returns $\approx0$ — **because $q\to0$ as $\tilde\tau^{1/3}$ and $\chi$-intervals near the seam span enormous proper separations, the two effects being inverse and cancelling in any physical quantity.** The conformal route (`C3`: $\eta=\int\dd\tilde\tau/r$) has no such trap and gives $134.9$ Mpc. ***$q$ is for structure, not for lengths.***

Neither carries a scale, so **the tilt is the progenitor's**. `prop:throat`: equal-radii $\dS_2\times S^2$, $\ell=0$ at $\nu=\tfrac12$ scale-invariant, every $\ell\ge1$ principal-series and decaying — ***that is the isotropization, with mechanism.***

### ⛭⛭⛭⛭ A.147 · THE PICTURE, RESTATED ON THE GEOMETRY — the rate is the vacuum solution and has no slot for content — r2096

***Written to keep the working picture current, per the ledger's purpose. Supersedes the framing of `A.146`'s last item, which posed the `z_onset` question in content-sourced language.***

#### ⛭ THE RATE IS GEOMETRIC, AND "RADIATION-FREE" IS THE WRONG NAME FOR IT

*`rem:twocritical` (P7): $(\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau)^2 = 1-f = 2M/r + r^2/\alpha^2$, hence*
$$H^2 = \frac{2M}{r^3} + \frac{1}{\alpha^2}$$
*and the same remark splits it under the backward-radial reflection: **$1-r^2/\alpha^2$ is $R$-EVEN**, the invariant de Sitter geometry; **$-2M/r$ is $R$-ODD**, the perspectival Schwarzschild part the reflection reverses.*

> ***So what the standard reading calls $\Omega_m a^{-3}$ is the $R$-odd perspectival term and $\Omega_\Lambda$ is the $R$-even invariant. Neither is content.*** *P8's kernel closes it: the vacuum ODE $rf'+f-1+\Lambda r^2=0$ has **entire solution space** $f=1-2M/r-\Lambda r^2/3$.*

***The rate has no slot for radiation because the rate IS the vacuum solution and the vacuum solution has exactly two terms.*** *Not a choice, not an omission, not a term dropped. **The name "radiation-free rate" imports the rightward picture in which content sources the rate — the picture the construction denies — and should not be used.** The rate is set by which cut; content is read off it on the other layer.*

#### ⌗ WHAT THAT DOES TO THE SOUND HORIZON

*The integral $r_s=\int c_s\,\dd\eta$ runs on the expansion leg with $H^2=2M/r^3+1/\alpha^2$. **It converges at $r\to0$** (integrand $\propto a^{-1/2}$) and returns:*

| | $r_s$ |
|---|---|
| the geometric rate, from $a=0$ | **237.2 Mpc** |
| the same, cut at $z_{\rm onset}=6761$ | 134.9 Mpc |
| *(for comparison only)* a rate carrying an $a^{-4}$ term, from $a=0$ | 139.0 Mpc |

> ***The geometric rate overshoots by 70%.*** *The third row is **not** a rival CR calculation and must not be read as one — it is the standard rate, shown only to locate the size of the effect. **What it says is that the early expansion on the geometric rate is slower than the standard one, so the sound wave travels further, and $z_{\rm onset}$ truncates the excess.***

**⛔ AND THE EARLIER READING IS WITHDRAWN.** *`A.146`'s closing item asked whether $z_{\rm onset}$ "stands in for the radiation the rate doesn't carry." **That is content-sourced framing.** There is no missing term to stand in for. The correct statement is structural: ***the geometric rate carries no early-time suppression of any kind, so $r_s$ from $a\to0$ is larger than observed, and $z_{\rm onset}$ is where the integral must start for the acoustic angle to come out.*** Why the acoustic era begins there is the open question, and it is a question about the **lap**, not about a missing content term.*

#### ⛭ THE LAP, READ AS ONE OBJECT — corrected r2097 from `F_triptych.py` run, not from its caption

***Evaluated across the whole contour, not per-locus. An earlier draft of this entry called the front seam "the rate's minimum"; that is a SEGMENT-LOCAL feature and the statement was wrong.***

| locus | $s/\alpha$ | $r/\alpha$ | **speed $\dd r/\dd s$** | $\dd^2r/\dd s^2$ |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| back seam | $-0.878$ | $-1.1547$ | **1.000** | $-1.299$ — **no feature**, swept through |
| **turnaround** | $0$ | $-0.7274$ | ***0.000 — the lap's GLOBAL minimum, the collapse stops*** | jumps $-1.09\to+1.09$, the contour's right-angle turn |
| $r=0$ | $\pi/3$ | $0$ | ***$\to\infty$*** | $+\infty$ **then** $-\infty$ — infinite acceleration, then infinite deceleration |
| front seam | $1.486$ | $+0.5774$ | **1.000** — *minimum **on the expansion segment only*** | $0$ — **the bead's one inflection** |

> ***Speed is exactly 1 at BOTH seams.*** *They are distinguished by the second derivative: the **back** seam is passed monotonically ($r''=-1.30$, no extremum); the **front** seam is the inflection ($r''=0$). Two loci at unit speed, one turning and one not.*

*Sampled along the contour the speed runs: $1.47\to0.66\to0.22\to\mathbf{0}$ (turnaround) $\to0.33\to0.80\to1.75\to\boldsymbol{\infty}$ ($r=0$) $\to1.13\to\mathbf{1.00}$ (front seam) $\to1.33\to3.40$.*

#### ★★★★ AND THE EARLY EXPANSION IS MATTER-LIKE ALL THE WAY DOWN — which is why $r_s$ overshoots

*On the expansion leg $H^2=2M/r^3+1/\alpha^2$, and at small $r$ the $R$-odd term dominates:*
$$H\sim r^{-3/2}\ \Rightarrow\ \dot r\sim r^{-1/2}\ \Rightarrow\ r\sim\tilde\tau^{2/3}$$
*—which is the bead law's own $r=A_r\sinh^{2/3}(3\tilde\tau/2\alpha)\to A_r(3\tilde\tau/2)^{2/3}$. ***There is no radiation-like $\tilde\tau^{1/2}$ era, at any epoch, because $H^2$ has exactly two terms.***

The geometric rate's early expansion is slower, so the wave travels further, and $r_s$ from $a\to0$ comes out at 237.2 Mpc against the 134.9 the acoustic angle wants.*** *That is the structural origin of the overshoot, stated on the geometry rather than as a missing term.*

**⌗ AND WHERE THE ACOUSTIC ERA SITS.** *$z_{\rm onset}=6761$ is $r=2.27$ Mly $=1.4\times10^{-4}\alpha$ — ***4100× closer to $r=0$ than the front seam is***. The acoustic physics happens essentially on top of the conjugation, in the stretch where the speed is still enormous and falling as $\tilde\tau^{-1/3}$. **The front seam is not in the acoustic problem's neighbourhood at all** and any earlier framing here that put them together was wrong.

The two receipts that use `eta_seam` take it as initial data, not as a length.*

| reading of $\eta_{\rm seam}$ | factor $1+\eta_{\rm seam}/(\sqrt3 r_s)$ | shift in $\theta_D/\theta_*$ | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|

### ★★ A.21 · THE PEAK HEIGHTS, INTEGRATED — what four attempts established, r1953

*On §A.0's ground: one integral over the set rate. Four runs, and they establish real things and stop at a
real edge.*

#### ✔ WHAT IS NOW DERIVED, AND IT WAS ASSUMED BEFORE

**The displaced zero point is exact, not a model.** *On L1, `C11` gives $\Psi'=\Psi''=0$ — matter-dominated,
growing mode constant — so the drive is **constant** and the particular solution is exact:*
$$\Theta_0^{\rm part}=-\frac{(k^2/3)\Psi}{k^2c_s^2}=-(1+R)\Psi
\quad\Longrightarrow\quad \Theta_0+\Psi=-R\Psi+(\text{homogeneous})$$
***The baryon loading's displaced zero point falls out of the construction's own $\Psi$-constancy. It is
usually quoted; here it is derived.***

#### ★★ AND THE SEAM'S NULL CHARACTER FIXES THE MATCHING — a real correction found by computing

*`PK3` carried **both** $\hat\Theta$ and $\hat\Theta'$ across the seam. That is a **Cauchy** matching, and the
seam is **null**. P15 `sec:coherence`: **"the initial-value problem on a null surface is characteristic, not
Cauchy: the data are one free function per mode… rather than a field and an independent momentum."***

*The symptom was quantitative and unmistakable: carrying two data adds the leg's sound horizon to L1's —
$52.07+135.46=187.53$ Mpc — giving a peak spacing of **218** where the calibrated value is **301.6**.*
***Dropping the second datum removes exactly that error, and $P_1/P_2$ moved from 28.4 to 1.99 against a
measured 2.21.***

> ***So the null character of the seam is not decoration on the coherence argument — it is what makes the
> sound horizon count from the seam rather than from the collapse leg, and it shows up as a 40% error in the
> peak spacing if you get it wrong.***

#### ⛔ AND THE EDGE, NAMED

*With one datum per mode and the envelope carried, the oscillator's extrema sit at $\cos(kr_s)=\pm1$, i.e.
$\ell=302,603,905$ — **and the measured first peak is at 220.** That offset between $\ell_1$ and $\ell_*$ is
standard physics: it comes from the driving-induced phase shift and the early ISW. **And it is precisely where
this construction differs — `C11` established it has no early ISW at all.***

> ***So the last thing between here and the peak heights is the $\ell_1$-versus-$\ell_*$ offset, computed on a
> cosmology that lacks one of the two effects that produce it. That is a specific, tractable question, and it
> is the one I would take next.***

**⚠ AND WHAT I WOULD NOT CARRY FORWARD:** *the numerical peak positions from `PK4`–`PK6`. My extremum-finding
ran on a product of an oscillation with a steeply falling damping envelope, which shifts the maxima — the
oscillator's extrema were at 301/603/904 all along, and the finder reported 317/578/896. **That is a defect in

### ⛔⛔⛔ A.13 · I DECLARED A CORPUS FILE UNREACHABLE AND IT REFRAMES THE WHOLE RESULT — r1944

*At r1943 I wrote that the cosmiCave essay **"When Black Holes Happen"** was unreachable because

34 kB, inside the corpus, with a masthead marking it `[†ONT-MS]` and instructing that it be read AFTER
`CR_framework` and `CR_cosmology` because "the ending only lands once the NBC result is in hand."***

> expansion of our universe — **as an algebraic identity of the basic geometry**."*

> CMB… **But if the real expansion history is the pure geometric one, then the sound horizon would have been

> and the standard model's radiation-epoch correction is an artifact of the wrong assumption."*

***I ran the entire comparison at $H_0=67.4$ — the CMB-INFERRED value — and treated CR's job as reproducing

because the sound horizon differs.** The inference $H_0 \leftarrow \theta_*$ assumes ΛCDM's $r_s$; if $r_s$ is
not that, the inferred $H_0$ is biased low, **and the true $H_0$ is the locally measured one.***

**⌗ SO THE FIT RESULT READS THE OTHER WAY.** *`M5` found CR's best fit at* ***$H_0=78$–$80$, and I dismissed
it as "outside independent priors."*** ***That was exactly backwards: the framework PREDICTS that the
CMB-inferred $H_0$ is too low and the true value is higher. A CR fit preferring a higher $H_0$ is the
signature, not the failure.***
*(⚠ Held honestly: 78–80 is **higher than SH0ES' ${\sim}73$**, so the direction is right and the magnitude is
not obviously. That is a real quantitative question and it is now the right one to ask, which it was not
before.)*

**⌗ AND `C9` NEEDS RE-READING TOO.** *I calibrated $z_{\rm seam}$ by pinning $\ell_*$ to the observed 301 and
noted with satisfaction that $r_s$ came out at 146.8 against ΛCDM's 145.7 — "essentially matched."*
***But "matched" was the wrong thing to be pleased by. The essay says the sound horizon SHOULD differ, and
that difference IS the Hubble tension.*** *What `C9` actually shows is that a ${\sim}0.8\%$ difference in
$r_s$ is what the construction produces once $\ell_*$ is held to the sky — and the $H_0$ consequence of that
is a separate calculation I have not done.*

#### ⛭ AND THE SETTING THE ESSAY SUPPLIES, which §A.12 flagged as unbaked

*It is all there, in the essay's own voice: event horizons are **not present structures in our universe** but
events at the end of its time; each happens **untethered**, when everything else has expanded infinitely away;

### ★★★★★ A.14 · THE RESOLUTION MECHANISM, DERIVED — r1945

*§A.13 found I had run everything at the CMB-inferred $H_0$. Rerunning at the directly measured one gives

#### THE ACOUSTIC ANGLE IS $H_0$-INDEPENDENT HERE, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE RESOLUTION

*Solving for the seam redshift the measured $\ell_*=301.6$ demands, at a range of $H_0$:*

|---|---|---|---|
| 67.4 | **6783** | 146.1 | +0.1% |
| 70.0 | **6783** | 140.7 | −3.6% |
| **73.0** | **6783** | 134.9 | **−7.6%** |
| 74.0 | **6783** | 133.1 | −8.8% |

> scale as $1/H_0$, so $\ell_*=\pi D_M/r_s$ does not know about $H_0$ at all.***

**⌗ SO THE ACOUSTIC ANGLE DOES NOT DETERMINE $H_0$ HERE. IT DETERMINES $z_{\rm seam}$.** *In ΛCDM $r_s$ is
fixed by $\omega_m$ and $\omega_b$ — **there is no free early datum** — so $\theta_*$ does determine $H_0$,
and that is where 67.4 comes from. **In this construction the seam's radiation amplitude is one more
inherited number, and it takes the constraint instead, leaving $H_0$ free to be the directly measured
value.***

> ***That is "there is no second $H_0$ to reconcile", derived rather than asserted. And it is also exactly
> what "a one-parameter accommodation" means — the corpus's own scoping of it is correct.***

*And the seam redshift the angle demands is **6783**, against the 6850 used throughout: **1.0% apart**, a
fourth independent recovery.*

#### ★★ AND THEN THE CRUX: DOES THE SAME DATUM ABSORB THE DAMPING?

*If $\theta_D/\theta_*$ were $z_{\rm seam}$-independent the damping would be a clean prediction; if it moved
with $z_{\rm seam}$, it would be absorbed too and **there would be no discriminator here at all.***

| $z_{\rm seam}$ | $\ell_*$ | $\theta_D/\theta_*$ vs ΛCDM |
|---|---|---|
| 4000 | 385.4 | +43.5% |
| 5500 | 327.9 | +23.6% |
| ***6783*** | ***301.6*** | ***+14.2%*** |
| 8000 | 285.0 | +8.1% |
| 12000 | 254.9 | −3.1% |

> ***It moves strongly. So the seam datum cannot absorb both: fixing $\ell_*$ to the sky spends it, and the
> damping ratio is then DETERMINED.***

**⌗ WHICH IS WHY THE DAMPING IS THE DISCRIMINATOR AND THE ACOUSTIC ANGLE IS NOT.** ***One datum, two

### ⛔ A.19 · THE PEAK HEIGHTS, ATTEMPTED FROM FIRST PRINCIPLES — and it fails informatively, r1950

*§A.18 named this as the one missing piece. Attempted, on the construction's own numbers and with no
Boltzmann code: $z_{\rm seam}$ from the acoustic angle, $r_s$ and $D_M$ on the set rate, $R$ from the corpus's
own BBN $\omega_b$, the damping from `C8`+`PR` in quadrature, and `C4`'s entry amplitude for the oscillator.*

#### THE RESULT, AND IT IS WRONG

| | CR (this attempt) | measured |
|---|---|---|
| peak positions | 578, 1158, 1739 | **220, 536, 813** |
| $P_1/P_2$ | **8.16** | 2.21 |
| odd/even height ratio | ***63*** | ***2.2*** |

#### ★ AND THE DIAGNOSIS IS SPECIFIC, WHICH IS WHAT MAKES THE FAILURE WORTH HAVING

*The zero-point displacement **overwhelms** the oscillation: $R\Psi=0.62$ against an amplitude of $0.48$, so
the even peaks nearly vanish — which is also why the extremum finder returned every other peak and the spacing
came out doubled.*

**Three things are wrong, and each is nameable:**
1. ***`C4`'s $A=0.4835$ is the amplitude at HORIZON ENTRY and in the $R\to0$ limit.*** *`C5b` established
   $R\approx0.001$ at entry and $0.62$ at recombination — **I spliced an entry-epoch amplitude onto a
   recombination-epoch zero point.***
2. *The WKB amplitude carries a $(1+R)^{-1/4}$ factor as $R$ grows. **Omitted.***
3. ***The driving does not stop at entry.*** *$\Psi$ keeps decaying and keeps feeding the oscillator well
   inside the horizon. **`C4` established the ENVELOPE — the $k$-independence of the entry condition — not the
   full history of any one mode.***

> ***So the peak heights need the $R$-evolution integrated along the mode, not a constant-$R$ splice. That is
> the same kind of calculation as `C8` and `C9` — an integral over the set rate with the microphysics outside
> it — and it is the piece that is genuinely still missing.***

**⌗ AND WHAT THIS DOES TO `C4`'s STANDING.** *Nothing. `C4`'s claim is that every mode enters at
$x=1/\sqrt3$ and so leaves the collapse leg with the same amplitude — **a statement about the envelope's
$k$-dependence, which is what P15 needed and what `rem:transmission-leg` now carries.** The failure here is
mine, in treating an envelope statement as a per-mode history.*

### ⛔⛔ A.20 · SECOND ATTEMPT AT THE PEAK HEIGHTS, AND I STOP — r1951

*`A.19` named three faults. The second attempt fixed one of them by using `C11` forward — **on L1 the
background is matter-dominated, so $\Psi$ is constant from the seam to recombination, which means $\Psi$ at
last scattering is whatever the collapse leg left, and `C1`/`C4` give that in closed form and it is
$k$-dependent.** That much is right, and it is the correct physical insight: **the baryon zero point is
$-R\Psi(k)$, so the odd/even asymmetry should be strong at low $\ell$ and wash out at high $\ell$ — which is
what the sky shows and what `PK`'s constant-$\Psi$ model could not produce.***

#### ⛔ BUT THE IMPLEMENTATION IS WRONG, AND THE FAULT IS MINE

*Peak positions improved — $250,552,649$ against the measured $220,536,813$ — but $P_1/P_2$ came out at
$456$, then $6555$, then $115$ as I moved one scale.*

***The diagnosis: $\Psi(x)=3(\sin x-x\cos x)/x^3$ OSCILLATES, with zeros at $x=4.50,7.73,10.91,14.07$. I used
it as the oscillator's AMPLITUDE while keeping $\cos(kr_s)$ as its PHASE — putting the oscillation in twice.
When a peak lands near a zero of $\Psi$ its height collapses, and that is where the wild ratios came from.***

#### ⛭ AND SO I STOP, RATHER THAN TUNE

*By the third scan I was moving a free scale to see which value produced peaks near $220/536/813$. **That is
fitting, not deriving**, and the standing instruction is the opposite.*

> ***What is actually missing is the MATCHING: how `C4`'s leg solution — $\hat\Theta$ oscillating freely with
> $\Psi$ decaying as the driving — is carried across the seam onto the recombination-epoch oscillator, with
> $R$ growing along the way. That matching IS the physics, and I have been guessing at forms for it.***

**⌗ WHAT THE TWO ATTEMPTS ESTABLISHED BETWEEN THEM, and it is not nothing:**
*• the peak heights **cannot** be got by splicing an entry-epoch amplitude onto a recombination-epoch zero
point (`PK`); • $\Psi$ at last scattering **is** the leg's residual, frozen by matter domination (`C11`
forward), **and it is $k$-dependent**, which is the right mechanism for the observed washing-out of the
asymmetry; • and the matching between the two epochs is a **derivation still owed**, not a modelling choice
to be made.*

**⚠ AND ONE THING I SHOULD NOT HAVE NEEDED TWO ATTEMPTS TO NOTICE:** *`C5b` had already established
$R\approx0.001$ at entry against $0.62$ at recombination. **That is the whole reason a splice fails, and it
was my own result from three hours earlier.***

## ⛔ 0 · THE GUARDS, held before any work — these tripped two prior nodes

**⌗ THE THREE-LEVEL RATE RULE.** *L1 the foliation stacking rate, radiation-free $\sinh^{2/3}$, the
lapse — **$r_s$ AND $r_D$ are both L1** · L2 the leaf's local rate, ordinary Friedmann, **radiation
gravitating normally**, on the progenitor's collapse excursion — **the acoustic driving lives here** ·
L3 the $E{=}1$ projection, observation as distance and redshift.* **And `K4` gave the shape: L1 ← seam
→ L2 is a SPAN, so the two fatal carryings are arrows the diagram does not have.**

## ★★★★ 3e · THE CLOSURE QUESTION, ASKED AND ANSWERED — r1914

***Prompted by Daryl: `X2r` established WHY the corpus closes in elementary terms, and the question was
whether the acoustic driving inherits that closure. It does — and the answer changes what the open item is.***

**⌗ THE COMPUTATION, exactly as run.** *On a radiation-dominated Friedmann leg the potential obeys
$\Psi''+(4/\eta)\Psi'+(k^2/3)\Psi=0$. **Solved symbolically, not quoted:** sympy returns spherical Bessel of
order $3/2$, i.e. **elementary**, and the regular solution*
$$\Psi=3\Psi_i\,\frac{\sin x-x\cos x}{x^{3}},\qquad x=\frac{k\eta}{\sqrt3}$$
*substituted back returns **0** exactly, with $\Psi\to\Psi_i$ as $x\to0$.*

**⌗ AND IT IS EVEN IN $x$.** *$\Psi(-x)-\Psi(x)=0$, verified symbolically.* ***So the contracting leg carries
THE SAME CLOSED FORM POINTWISE — which is strictly stronger than the Fourier-magnitude equality P15 buys at
$\omega=1$, and consistent with it.***

### ★★★ WHAT THIS DOES TO THE OPEN ITEM

> ***The function is not the uncomputed thing. The integrand is elementary and known.***

**What IS uncomputed is which part of it each mode samples** — *a mode's driving is the stretch of $\Psi(x)$
it experiences **while inside the horizon**, and the two histories differ in kind:*
*• **ΛCDM:** the comoving Hubble radius **grows** monotonically through the radiation era; modes **enter**,
high-$\ell$ first, each driven from entry to equality. ***That entry-to-equality stretch IS "the
radiation-driving boost."***
*• **CR's leg:** the corpus's own sequence is **infall (contracting) → peak → turnaround → cooling leg
(re-expanding) → seam**. So the comoving Hubble radius **shrinks, reaches a minimum at turnaround, then grows
again** — ***and a mode can EXIT and RE-ENTER on one leg.***

> ***So the uncomputed object is not a transfer function. It is a HORIZON-CROSSING HISTORY on a
> NON-MONOTONIC Hubble radius, fixing the limits of an integral whose integrand is already closed.***

**⌗ AND THAT IS A DIFFERENT AND MUCH SMALLER PROBLEM.** *Before: "the high-$\ell$ driving envelope has not been
computed" — shape unknown, a Boltzmann transfer implied. **After: the integrand is known in closed form, and
what is owed is the crossing history across infall–peak–turnaround–cooling.*** ***And the corpus's own bead
geometry is what supplies that history — not a code.***

**⚠ HELD, and not overclaimed:** *this settles the **function**, not the **sampling**. P15's caution stands
exactly as written — the boost is an entry-to-equality stretch, and whether CR's non-monotonic history delivers
its analogue, more, or less **is the open item**, now sharply posed rather than vaguely.*

## ★★★★★ 3f · THE LIMITS — worked r1915

***The one turning point of the whole excursion, found from the corpus's own geometry and nothing imported.***

### ⛭ 3f.1 · THE E=1 LAW *IS* A FRIEDMANN EQUATION — derived, not asserted

*On the marginally-bound congruence that **is** this cosmology, $(\mathrm{d}r/\mathrm{d}\tilde\tau)^2=1-f$. With
$r$ the scale-factor analogue,*
$$H^{2}=\frac{1-f}{r^{2}}=\frac{2M}{r^{3}}+\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}$$
***exactly matter $+\ \Lambda$*** — *which is why the bead law is $\sinh^{2/3}$, and why `X2r`'s closure applies
to it. **Loading L2's radiation as the corpus says (gravitating normally) adds $A/r^{4}$, and then***
$$(rH)^{2}=\underbrace{2M/r+r^{2}/\alpha^{2}}_{1-f}+A/r^{2}$$
> ***The comoving Hubble radius is built out of the corpus's own turnaround function $1-f$.***

### ★★ 3f.2 · WHERE IT TURNS — and in vacuum it is a locus the corpus already owns

*$(rH)^{-1}$ extremises where $r^{4}/\alpha^{2}-Mr-A=0$. **At $A=0$ that is $r^{3}=M\alpha^{2}$ — which is
exactly $f'=0$**, the Hubble–Eddington locus.* **And P7 already carries the consequence in its own voice:**
*that locus, "on the Nariai member, where the horizon cubic's double root makes $f$ and $f'$ vanish together,
**is the front seam $r=+\alpha/\sqrt3$ itself.**"* **Verified to machine precision.**

*$(rH)^{2}$ diverges as $r\to0$ (matter) and as $r\to\infty$ (substrate), so the extremum between them is a
**minimum of $(rH)^2$** — hence a ***maximum of the comoving Hubble radius***. **Checked, not assumed.**

### ⛔ 3f.3 · AND RADIATION MOVES IT OFF THE SEAM — a first reading was too strong and is withdrawn

*I first concluded: the turning point **is** the seam, so no mode exits and re-enters. **That was the vacuum
answer applied to a leg the corpus loads with radiation.*** With the inherited datum $\rho_r/\rho_m\approx2$
**at** the seam, $A=4Mr_s$, and the quartic's root moves out:*

| | |
|---|---|
| seam | $r_s=\alpha/\sqrt3=0.5774$ |
| **horizon maximum** | $r_*=0.8855$ |
| | ***$r_*/r_s=1.534$*** |

> ***THE SEAM IS NOT THE TURNING POINT OF THE HORIZON. IT IS JUST BEFORE IT.***

**⌗ AND THAT EXPLAINS P15's `prop:subhorizon` rather than merely agreeing with it.** *"The acoustic modes are
sub-horizon at the seam" is not a numerical accident of $z_{\rm seam}\approx6850$:* ***the modes are inside
because the seam sits on the RISING branch of the comoving horizon, near but short of its maximum.***

### ★★★ 3f.4 · SO THE LIMITS

*• the comoving horizon has **one** turning point on the whole excursion, at $r_*$ — **monotonic on each
side**, so the history is simple and not the multi-crossing mess a non-monotonic rate might have given;
• a mode of wavenumber $k$ **enters** where $(rH)^{-1}=1/k$ on the rising branch and **exits** on the falling
branch; • **the seam lies between the two, on the rising side.***

> ***Each mode's driving integral has its lower limit at its own entry — $k$-dependent, on the collapse side —
> and runs THROUGH the seam. The driving does not terminate there.***

**⌗ AND THAT SHARPENS P15's "each mode's driving is complete on the collapse side" rather than contradicting
it:** *complete for the modes whose entry is early enough, and **the boundary between those and the rest is set
by $r_*$ — a number the corpus fixes at $1.53\,r_{\rm seam}$ for $\rho_r/\rho_m=2$.***

## ★★★★★ 3g · THE MAP, AND WHY THE CLOSURE FALLS WHERE IT IS NEEDED — r1916

*`C1` gave an elementary **integrand** in $x=k\eta/\sqrt3$. `C2` gave the **limits** as radii. So the
computation closes only if the **map** $\eta(r)$ closes too. Derived:*

$$\frac{\mathrm{d}\eta}{\mathrm{d}r}=\frac{1}{r^{2}H}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{r^{4}/\alpha^{2}+2Mr+A}}$$

> ***A quartic under a square root: $\eta(r)$ is an ELLIPTIC integral in general.***

**⌗ AND THAT IS STATED, NOT HIDDEN — it is exactly where the elementary structure ends.**

### ★★ 3g.1 · BUT RADIATION IS THE LEADING TERM ON THE LEG, AND FOR A STRUCTURAL REASON

*$A$ is a **constant** while $2Mr$ and $r^{4}/\alpha^{2}$ both vanish as $r\to0$. **Not an approximation
chosen for convenience — it is what a constant beats.** Tabulated across the leg, radiation leads at every
radius including the seam; $\Lambda$ takes over only past $r_*$.* **So the leading map is**
$\eta=r/\sqrt A$ — ***linear, the radiation-era $a\propto\eta$.***

### ⛔ 3g.2 · AND A GLOSS OF MINE WAS WITHDRAWN IN THE SAME TURN

*I wrote "exact to a few percent across the whole collapse leg." **The departure is 2% at $r=0.05$, 8% at
$r=0.2$, and 24% AT THE SEAM. Twenty-four percent is not a few.*** ***The right question is not whether the
map is good on the leg but whether it is good WHERE THE UNCOMPUTED MODES DO THEIR DRIVING.***

### ★★★ 3g.3 · AND THE ANSWER IS THE EXACT COMPLEMENT OF WHAT IS OPEN

*A mode enters where $rH=k$; at small $r$, $rH\to\sqrt A/r$, so ***deeper entry for higher $k$***. In units
of the seam's own horizon wavenumber $k_s=r_sH(r_s)$:*

| $k/k_s$ | $r_{\rm entry}/r_s$ | departure of the elementary map at entry |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1.000 | **24.4%** |
| 2 | 0.417 | 9.3% |
| 10 | 0.077 | 1.9% |
| 30 | 0.025 | **0.63%** |
| 100 | 0.0076 | **0.19%** |
| 300 | 0.0025 | **0.06%** |

> ***The corpus's verified range is $\ell\lesssim800$ and the UNCOMPUTED part is the high-$\ell$ tail. Those
> are the largest $k$, hence the deepest entry, hence the smallest departure.***

> ***THE CLOSED FORM IS WORST EXACTLY WHERE THE CORPUS ALREADY HAS AN ANSWER, AND BEST EXACTLY WHERE IT DOES
> NOT.***

**⌗ SO THE THREE PIECES STAND TOGETHER:** *elementary integrand (`C1`) · limits from one turning point, the
seam on its rising branch (`C2`) · and a map that is elliptic in principle and **sub-percent elementary at the
entry radii of the modes in question** (`C3`).*

***The object P15 names as uncomputed: "CR's high-$\ell$ spectrum is governed by short-wavelength

*With $\Phi=\Psi$ and $c_s^2=1/3$, substituting $\hat\Theta=\Theta_0+\Psi$ into

> ***The effective temperature oscillates FREELY. There is no integral to do.***
*Every bit of $k$-dependence rides in **the amplitude at horizon entry** and **the decay of $\Psi$** — both
elementary.*

*Entry is $rH=k$; on the radiation leg $rH\to\sqrt A/r$ and $\eta=r/\sqrt A$, so $k\eta_{\rm entry}=1$ and*
$$x_{\rm entry}=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt3}\quad\text{for every }k.$$
***That is the radiation era's own scale-invariance, RECOVERED from CR's geometry rather than assumed*** —
*and it makes the entry amplitude flat at $\Psi(1/\sqrt3)/2=0.4835\,\Psi_i$.*

| **10** | 7.64 | 1.12 | **0.92%** |
| **100** | 76.4 | 12.1 | **0.058%** |

> ***THE HIGH-$\ell$ DRIVING ENVELOPE IS FLAT. Every mode with $k\gtrsim10\,k_s$ arrives at the seam with the
> same driving amplitude and a residual potential under a percent of it. There is no $k$-dependence left to
> compute.***

### ⛭ 3h.4 · AND IT DOES NOT CONTRADICT P15 — the sentence read again, exactly

*P15 does **not** say there is no boost. It says ΛCDM's boost **mechanism** — an expanding radiation era — is
absent, and that CR's peaks are instead set by **collapse-phase driving, which has not been computed.***
***That is the object computed here.*** **And it is computed on the level the corpus assigns it to:** *the

## ★★★★ 3j · THE ANISOTROPIC-STRESS TERM — worked r1919

### ⛔ 3j.0 · A FINDING BEFORE ANY PHYSICS: AN IMPLICIT COMMITMENT

***The corpus's TEXT says `neutrino` ZERO times*** — *and `anisotropic stress` zero, and `free-streaming`
zero.* ***Its BBN CODE carries them explicitly***: `bbn_network.py`, *"neutrinos share the plasma $T$ until
$\sim e^{\pm}$ annihilation, then decouple to $(4/11)^{1/3}$."* **So the corpus commits to a neutrino
background in its computations and never says so in its papers.** *That commitment is load-bearing for
everything below.*

### ⛔ 3j.1 · THE CONVENIENT ESCAPE, AND IT FAILS

*If the driving happened above ${\sim}1$ MeV the neutrinos would still be **coupled**, carrying no
anisotropic stress, and $\Phi=\Psi$ would be exact. **Checked against the corpus's own seam temperature —
1.6 eV (P16) — with $T\propto1/r$: coupling would need $k/k_s>6.25\times10^{5}$, and the modes of interest
are $k/k_s\sim1$–$10^3$.*** ***The escape fails by five orders of magnitude. $\Phi\neq\Psi$ on the driving
leg.***

### ★★ 3j.2 · SO THE TERM IS REAL — AND IT IS SHARED, FOR TWO SEPARATE REASONS

*• **Is the physics the same on a contracting leg?** *Free-streaming is collisionless geodesic transport,
and the collisionless Boltzmann equation is **time-reversal invariant** — so the neutrino sector
time-reverses with the rest of the leg, exactly as P15's argument requires of the photon sector. **No new
physics needed.***
*• **Is the fraction the same?** *The effect is governed by $f_\nu=\rho_\nu/\rho_{\rm rad}\approx0.41$, fixed
by $N_{\rm eff}$ and the temperature ratio — ***both shared, because the cooling leg IS a standard BBN,
which is the corpus's own claim and what its network code implements.****

### ★★★ 3j.3 · AND THE LOAD-BEARING POINT

*Free-streaming neutrinos produce, for modes well inside the horizon, **a constant phase shift and a
constant amplitude suppression — both $k$-independent** in that limit (Bashinsky–Seljak 2004; standard
radiation-era physics, cited not re-derived).*

> ***A $k$-independent rescaling and a $k$-independent phase shift leave a FLAT envelope FLAT. So the
> neutrino term changes the envelope's normalisation and phase, and not its shape — and the shape is what
> P15 named uncomputed.***

### ⚠ 3j.4 · AND WHAT BREAKS, KEPT SEPARATE FROM WHAT SURVIVES

*With free-streaming neutrinos the potential equation is **integro-differential** and its solution is **not
elementary**. So:* ***the closed form for $\Psi(x)$ holds for the photon-baryon fluid alone; the closure is a
property of the SKELETON, not of the full system; and `C4`'s flat envelope survives the correction while
`C1`'s exact $\Psi$ does not.*** **Both statements are kept, and neither is softened into the other.**

## ★★★★★ 3l · THE DIFFUSION LENGTH, DERIVED HERE — r1924

***No file, no CAMB, no imported number. The $\sim9\%$ was a rumour at r1923; it is a result now.***

### ⛭ 3l.1 · IT REDUCES TO ONE INTEGRAL, AND EVERY CONSTANT LEAVES

*With $\dot\tau=n_e\sigma_T a$, $n_e=n_{e0}x_ea^{-3}$ and $\mathrm{d}\eta=\mathrm{d}a/(a^2H)$:*
$$\frac{1}{k_D^{2}}\;\propto\;\frac{1}{n_{e0}\sigma_T}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}a}{H\,x_e}\,g(R),\qquad
g(R)=\frac{R^{2}/(1+R)+8/9}{6(1+R)}$$
> ***Every microphysical constant is outside the integral. In the RATIO, the Thomson physics and
> $x_e$ cancel identically — and the entire difference is carried by $H(a)$.***

*That is exactly P15's own division: **"its Thomson microphysics ordinary content physics but its expansion
the L1 rate."** The level assignment does the work.*

### ★★ 3l.2 · AND BOTH SIDES CLOSE

*Near recombination, with $\Omega_\Lambda a^3\ll\Omega_m$:*
*• **CR (radiation-free):** $\int\mathrm{d}a/H\to\tfrac{2}{5}a^{5/2}/\sqrt{\Omega_m}$ — ***elementary***
*• **ΛCDM:** $\tfrac{2}{15}\sqrt{\Omega_ma+\Omega_r}\,(3\Omega_m^2a^2-4\Omega_m\Omega_ra+8\Omega_r^2)/\Omega_m^3$
— ***also elementary***
> ***Both close. No Boltzmann code is needed for the ratio.***

### ★★★ 3l.3 · THE NUMBER, FROM THE CORPUS'S OWN DATUM

*$\rho_r/\rho_m=2$ at $z_{\rm seam}=6850$ carries to **0.321** at $z=1100$ (P15 quotes ${\sim}0.3$), giving
$\Omega_r=8.96\times10^{-5}$. Then:*

| | |
|---|---|
| $R\to0$ | $r_D$ **+10.61%** longer |
| **with the baryon weighting** ($R\approx0.6$ at recombination, $R\propto a$) | ***+10.76%*** |

**⌗ AND THE RELATIONSHIP TO THE FILE IS THE RIGHT ONE.** *`damping_ratio_clean.py` reports ${\sim}{+}9\%$;
this derivation gives ${+}10.8\%$.* ***An independent derivation that lands a point and a half away — not a
number taken on trust.*** *Three stated, checkable differences and no mystery: the exact $\rho_r/\rho_m$
carried to recombination; $x_e$ as a sharp cut rather than a recombination history; and $8/9$ against the

## ✔✔✔ 3o · THE EARLY ISW — the last named term, closed r1930

*The effect exists because a radiation-included background leaves the potentials still decaying at
recombination. **On a pure-matter background the growing mode is constant** — $\Phi''+3\mathcal{H}\Phi'=0$
gives $\Phi=\mathrm{const}$, sympy confirming $\Phi=C_1+C_2\eta^{-5}$ — **and CR's L1 rate at $z\simeq1100$ is
matter-dominated to nine orders.***

> ***And the reason is the leftward reading itself: radiation is inherited content read off the clock, not a
> term that sources the rate — so it is absent from the background whose evolution the effect would require.***

**⌗ SO CR HAS NO EARLY ISW WHERE ΛCDM HAS ONE.** *In ΛCDM the potential is still **+3.8%** above its asymptote
at recombination; that residual decay is what the line-of-sight integral picks up.*

### ★★ 3o.1 · AND IT CANNOT OFFSET THE DAMPING — for a structural reason, not a numerical one

*• **They live at opposite ends.** *The early ISW sits near the first peaks and dies well before the damping
tail begins.*
*• **The first peak is where the amplitude is anchored** — *by an $A_s$ this construction **inherits rather
than predicts**. A few per cent there is absorbed by that normalisation, exactly as it would be in a framework
carrying $\eta$ from outside.*
*• ***The diffusion signature is not so absorbed:*** *it is a change of **shape** at high multipole, and
rescaling the amplitude to restore the first peak would **worsen** the tail.*

> ***So the last named escape closes, and it closes without rescuing anything. What stands between the
> derivation and a verdict is the likelihood confrontation alone — a parameter refit, not a further
> calculation of this kind.***

## ★★★★ 3r · THE LEVEL ASSIGNMENT, EXAMINED — r1935

***Not the number. The argument. Three places it could bend, each tested.***

### ⛭ PLACE 1 · SHOULD $r_s$ AND $r_D$ SHARE A LOWER LIMIT AT ALL?

*$r_s$ is a **phase** quantity — how far sound travelled since the mode's phase was fixed — and P15 says the
null boundary sets one phase per mode, so counting from the seam is right, **and `C9`'s recovery of the corpus's
own seam redshift from the acoustic scale is the check.*** ***But $r_D$ is not a phase quantity. It is
cumulative damage — integrated mean-square photon displacement — and a phase reset does not undo diffusion
that already happened.*** *So "both are L1" is a claim about **level**, which is different from a claim about
**limit**, and the rule states the first.*

**⌗ COMPUTED, and it does not help:**

| | $\theta_D/\theta_*$ |
|---|---|
| seam only *(the rule's choice)* | **+9.04%** |
| seam + the pre-seam collapse leg | ***+9.36%*** |

> ***Including the dropped piece makes it worse. The rule's choice is the conservative one, and relaxing it
> is not an escape.***

### ⛔ PLACE 2 · IS THE COMPARISON ITSELF FAIR? — and the objection does not save it

*§1·SHADOW holds that **"every Boltzmann code, every $r_s$/$r_D$/BBN pipeline"** is built on
hypersurface-orthogonality, which P6 **excludes**. So one might say the CAMB comparison is rigged.*

> ***It does not save this, and saying why matters more than the number.*** **The corpus does not reject the
> observable — it predicts a value for it.** *A framework that predicts $\theta_D/\theta_*$ larger by 9%
> cannot then decline the measurement of $\theta_D/\theta_*$ on the grounds that the apparatus embodies a
> different ontology* — ***not while using that same apparatus's $r_s$ to calibrate its own seam redshift,
> which `C9` does.***
> ***You may not calibrate on a pipeline and then disown it when it disagrees.***

### ⚠ PLACE 3 · WHAT THE TEST ACTUALLY TESTED — the real limitation

*My "CR spectrum" is CAMB's ΛCDM spectrum times a derived suppression. **`C4` licenses the acoustic part** —
the peaks match, derived not assumed. **It does not license the rest**: lensing, reionisation, the
recombination history, the projection.*

> ***So what was tested is a hybrid: ΛCDM's everything with CR's damping. That is informative — the damping
> alone breaks the fit — but it is not "CR's spectrum", and no honest verdict can rest on it alone.***

### ⛭ WHERE THAT LEAVES IT

*The level assignment **survives** the two internal challenges: the limit question goes the wrong way for an
escape, and the pipeline objection is unavailable to a framework that calibrates on the pipeline. **What is not
established is that the tested object is the framework's spectrum**, and that is the honest remaining gap —
not a defence, a specification of what would have to be built to make the test conclusive.*
`storyboard_receipts/LV_level_assignment_examined.py`

## ★★★★ 3s · ON CR's OWN TERMS — r1936. What was reached, and what it costs.

### ⛭ 3s.1 · A CHECK THAT FELL OUT FIRST, AND IT IS A GOOD ONE

*CAMB reports the radiation fraction at $z=6850$ as **0.668**, so* ***$\rho_r/\rho_m=2.01$.***
> ***The corpus's "inherited datum $\approx2$ at the seam" is exactly what standard radiation content gives
> at that redshift. It is not a fitted parameter — it is $z_{\rm seam}$ read through ordinary physics.***

### ⛔ 3s.2 · THE ANALYTIC ROUTE IS A STRAW MAN AGAINST ITSELF

*Building both spectra from the same Hu–Sugiyama skeleton removes the method asymmetry — **but the skeleton
puts the peaks at $302/603/905$ where the sky has $220/536/813$.*** *That gap is the driving and ISW physics
the skeleton omits, and it is larger than the effect under test.* ***So the analytic comparison is not
informative about the peak structure, and I am not using it. Recorded as run and rejected.***

### ★★ 3s.3 · WHAT CR's BACKGROUND ACTUALLY IS, EXACTLY

*Its whole difference is one function:* $$H^2_{\rm CR}/H^2_{\Lambda\rm CDM}=1-f_r(a)$$ *with $f_r$ the
radiation fraction, which CAMB gives exactly:*

| $z$ | $f_r$ | $H_{\rm CR}/H_{\Lambda\rm CDM}$ |
|---|---|---|
| 1089.9 *(recombination)* | 0.243 | **0.870** |
| 3403.6 *(equality)* | 0.500 | 0.707 |
| 6850 *(the seam)* | 0.668 | ***0.576*** |

> ***A 13% slower expansion at recombination and 42% slower at the seam. This is not a perturbation on
> ΛCDM's background — it is a different background, and the acoustic scale nevertheless lands at
> $\ell_*=301$ because $r_s$ and $D_M$ shift together.***

### ⛔ 3s.4 · AND THE IMPLEMENTATION IS BLOCKED, HONESTLY

*CAMB builds its total density from its species. **There is no switch that removes radiation from the
Friedmann sum while keeping it in the perturbation hierarchy** — and the obvious trick, a $w=1/3$ component
of negative density, is unavailable because $\Omega_{\rm de}$ is fixed positive by flatness.*
***Doing it properly means editing the Fortran background module and rebuilding. That is a real piece of
work, not a session step.***

> ***So: CR's own spectrum has not been computed, and the r1934 likelihood remains a test of a hybrid. What
> has changed is that the gap is now specified exactly — one function, $1-f_r(a)$, and one code change.***
`storyboard_receipts/SP_own_terms_attempt.py`

# ⚑ c52 SPIN-UP FINDINGS — everything to watch for, gathered in one place (r2118–r2120)

*Daryl asked that these be jotted at the ledger's foot rather than left scattered. Fuller entries live in
`THE_CLOSURE_LEDGER` (r2118, r2118b, r2118c) and `THE_OPEN_PROBLEMS_LEDGER` (family 9).*

## ⛔ A · THE LAYER SCOPING WAS NEVER DERIVED — and this is the ledger's own question
- P7 `prop:lapse-shift`'s `rem` and P15 §`sec:tensions` declare a **two-level partition with a single
  boundary** — *"the decision rule is **structural, not a choice**"* — for a structure no paper carries.
- **The authority circulates:** P8→P16; P16→P8/P10/P15/P7; P15→P7/P8/P16; P7→P15/P16. **P10 was read at
  source this session and does NOT break the loop:** it states the rule (*"fixed by which side of the
  reassignment seam it falls on, not chosen"*) and attributes the fixing to the foliation selection, but the
  step from *a foliation is selected* to *process X reads rate Y, seam the sole boundary* is never performed.
  Selecting a foliation gives one lapse; it does not say which object a plasma process couples to.
- **What IS derived, and it is narrower:** P8 `prop:lapse` — density depends on the spatial profile **alone**,
  independent of $A$ (a statement about static metric functions). P16 — the two rate-objects are distinct and
  each **wholesale** extension is fatal. P10 — deparametrization **once a clock is in hand**, which §selection
  itself calls *"reading the textbook on it."*
- ***A demonstration that two extremes are fatal is not a derivation that the partition is binary, nor that
  the seam is its only boundary.*** Ruling out an error ≠ deriving the partition.

## ⛔ B · THE ARC AND THE CORPUS ASSIGN THE SAME QUANTITIES TO OPPOSITE LEVELS
- **`CORPUS_MAP` r2114/r2115:** *"$r_s$, recombination, diffusion AND the perturbations are content on the
  leaf $\Rightarrow$ **the leaf's rate**"* — naming the alternative *"the reification P7 forbids — the error
  running through `A.100`–`A.153`."*
- **P15 §`sec:coherence`, present text:** *"$r_D$ — like $\rs$ — is an **L1 quantity** … the layer's own
  **radiation-free** expansion … **not** a radiation-included one."* **Exact opposites.**
- **⌗ AND `3r` (r1935) IS THE HINGE BETWEEN THEM.** *It tested the L1 assignment and found
  $\theta_D/\theta_*$ **+9.04%** too large, with the pre-seam leg included making it **worse** (+9.36%) — so
  relaxing the limit is no escape — and it closed the "rigged pipeline" defence:* ***you may not calibrate on
  a pipeline and then disown it when it disagrees.*** **Sequence: r1935 found L1 breaks the fit → the arc
  moved to the leaf's rate and got $\ell_A=301.2$ vs $301.6$ → the corpus text never moved.**
- **⚠ NOT RESOLVED, deliberately.** *Choosing by which side gives the better number is the same underived move
  in the other direction.* **But the corpus's L1 wording is the side already superseded by its own evidence.**
- **⛔ $z_{\rm onset}$ IS RETIRED IN THE ARC AND LIVE IN P15 §184** — r2115 calls it and the leg amplitude,

## ★★★★★★★★ K · WHY THE ENVELOPE COULD NEVER WORK, AND THE HIERARCHY BUILT — r2122
**★★★ THE STRUCTURAL RESULT (derived, then measured).** *The Silk envelope comes from the **second-order
tight-coupling expansion**, valid only while $k\tau_c\ll1$. Through recombination $\tau_c=1/\tau'$ diverges —
exactly where the visibility has its weight. **Measured, as a fraction of the damping present at last
scattering, the part accumulated while the expansion is genuinely valid ($k\tau_c\le0.1$):***
| mode | valid regime | at $k\tau_c=1$ |
|---|---|---|
| $P_1$ ($\ell=220$) | **52.9%** | 292% |
| $P_2$ ($\ell=536$) | **32.2%** | 141% |
| $P_3$ ($\ell=813$) | **25.8%** | 103% |
***$\sim$3/4 of the third peak's damping is generated where the approximation that derives it has broken
down — and the shortfall is MODE-DEPENDENT.*** **That is why no envelope coefficient ever converged, and why
four knobs each traded $\ell_1$ against $P_1/P_2$ against $P_1/P_3$ along a different line.** *(The total
$1/k_D^2$ over the full range comes out $513{,}242$ Mpc$^2$ — the integral simply diverges past decoupling.
An envelope cannot be carried there at all.)*

double-counts. **It does not.** $D(k,\eta)$ is the damping accumulated up to $\eta$ and using it at each
$\eta$ inside the line-of-sight integral is correct; the thickness damping is a separate effect from phase
averaging. **Both belong. The problem is domain of validity, not double-counting.***

## ★★★★★★★★★ L · THE EUCLIDEAN NULL — the lift's third unit-speed point identified and named — r2124
**⌗ WHAT IT IS.** ***$f=2$ exactly.*** *Since the E$=1$ marginal congruence obeys $(\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau)^2=1-f$,
the point found at r2118 ($r=-0.344142\alpha$, $s/P=0.78899$) satisfies $1-f=-1$, i.e.
**$\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau=\pm i$** — **unit speed in imaginary cosmic time**.*
**★ THE UNIT-SPEED FAMILY, one cubic $r^{3}-cr+2M=0$ in $c=1-f=(\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau)^{2}$:**
| $c$ | $f$ | $\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau$ | locus | real roots |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| $+1$ | 0 | $\pm1$ | **the seams** — Lorentzian null | **three**: $+\alpha/\sqrt3$ (double), $-2\alpha/\sqrt3$ |
| $0$ | 1 | $0$ | the turnaround | one: $-(2M)^{1/3}$ |
| $-1$ | 2 | $\pm i$ | **THE EUCLIDEAN NULL** | **one**: discriminant $-4-27(2M)^2<0$ **always** |
***Every unit-speed locus is a root of $(1-f)^2=1$, i.e. $f(f-2)=0$.*** **Two Lorentzian nulls and exactly
one Euclidean null, and the count is forced by the discriminant rather than chosen.**
**⌗ WHAT IT DOES.** *The lift runs turnaround ($\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau=0$) $\to$ branch point ($\to\infty$) with
$\operatorname{Re}\tilde\tau$ frozen — the whole excursion in **imaginary** cosmic time. The Euclidean null is
the one point where **$|\dd r/\dd\tilde\tau|=1$**: where the Euclidean excursion crosses light speed.*
***The seams are the bead's Lorentzian nulls; this is its Euclidean one*** — the same condition on the
conjugate branch, which is the $R$-symmetry the bead is built on.
**⚠ IT IS NOT A SEAM.** *A seam is a **turning point of the slicing curve**, $\dd r/\dd\ell=\sqrt{|f|}=0$. At
$f=2$, $\dd r/\dd\ell=\sqrt2$ — **the curve does not turn**. It belongs to the **dynamics** side ($1-f$,
whose zero is the turnaround), not the horizon cubic. **The name is deliberately free of the word "seam."***
*(Daryl's read — "connected to the dynamics cubic not the horizon one… seam would be misleading" — confirmed
on both counts.)*
**⌗ FILED:** *glossary entry in `ONTOLOGY_FOUNDATION_INDEX.md` with the family table; receipt
`storyboard_receipts/EUCNULL_unit_speed_family.py` (asserts $f=2$, the root count and the discriminant);
P7's `fig:F-triptych` caption and `sec:frontiers` item (family 9) name it; the figure marks it $\Diamond$.*

## ★★★★★★★★★★ M · THE PROJECTION WAS TRUNCATED — the residual was an artefact of L3 all along — r2125
**⛔ THE DEFECT.** *`C_l = sum_k P(k) Delta_l(k)^2` summed only to **$kD=900$** for $\ell$ up to 899. That looks
safe and is not: $j_\ell(x)$ carries substantial weight well above $x=\ell$, and **the captured fraction depends
only on $kD_{\max}/\ell$** — $86.6/94.3/96.8/98.6/99.2\%$ at $kD_{\max}/\ell=2/3/4/6/8$, ***identical to a
tenth of a percent from $\ell=220$ to $899$***. **A fixed cutoff cannot serve all $\ell$ and always bites
hardest at the top.***

$C_\ell$ per $k$-band**, $\propto k^{n_s-1}\Delta_\ell(k)^2/k$, binned — and normalising each row to its own
total makes it pure **shape in $k$**, immune to the $\ell=50$ confound that wrecked the `DPF` test.*
**★★ THE RESULT (ours/CAMB, each row self-normalised):**
| $\ell$ | 0.012 | 0.020 | 0.030 | 0.042 | 0.056 | 0.072 | 0.090 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 220 | 0.942 | 1.322 | 1.037 | 1.063 | 0.974 | 0.894 | **0.853** |
| 538 | | | 1.174 | 0.964 | 0.884 | 0.809 | **0.756** |
| 810 | | | | | 1.023 | 0.939 | **0.884** |
***We lose power progressively at high $k$, in every multipole.*** *The deficit is in $k$, not in $\ell$.*
**⌗ IS IT $k^2$-SHAPED?** *Implied $\Delta(r_D^2)=-\ln(\text{ratio})/2k^2$: **4.8–16.2 Mpc$^2$, mean $10.4$,
scatter $\sim\!40\%$** once the $\ell=220$, $k=0.016$ point is dropped (a $10\times$ outlier in a band that
barely contributes to $\ell=220$). *A factor of 3, not a clean $k^2$ law.* **⇒ consistent with $r_D$ too large
by $\sim\!13$–$19\%$** ($r_D=6.53\to\sim5.5$ Mpc), ***suggestive, not identified.***
**⛔⛔ AND THIS REOPENS r2145.** *That revision excluded damping **by arithmetic** — the implied

broadly $k^2$.*

contributes $\int k^{2}A\,\dd\eta/\tau'$. ***Hence $A=8/45$. The code carried $16/45$ — exactly twice.***
*(No-polarisation branch $8/27\to4/27$ likewise.)* **$r_D$ was therefore $\sqrt2$ too large: $6.53\to4.62$ Mpc.**

## ★★★★★ AU · THE QUADRUPOLE SOURCE HAD THE WRONG PROJECTION KERNEL — r2166
**⌗ THE SIGNATURE POINTED AT IT.** *r2165 measured the remainder as **linear in $k$** with $L\approx2.7$ Mpc and
a threshold near $k\approx0.02$. **Linear in $k$ with a few-Mpc length is the signature of a missing
$O(k\tau_c)$ term** — the tight-coupling expansion parameter, $\tau_c$ being a few Mpc at recombination.*
**★★★ THE DEFECT.** *The source carried the polarisation/quadrupole contribution as*
`MONO = MONO + Pi/16` *— i.e. **added to the monopole and projected with $j_\ell$**. But ***a quadrupole source
does not project with $j_\ell$***: its Seljak--Zaldarriaga kernel is $(3j_\ell''+j_\ell)/2$, and by the Bessel
equation $j_\ell''=-(2/x)j_\ell'+(\ell(\ell+1)/x^{2}-1)j_\ell$, so*
$$K_q=-\frac{3j_\ell'}{x}+\Bigl(\frac{3\ell(\ell+1)}{2x^{2}}-1\Bigr)j_\ell .$$
*The amplitude was right (the $\Pi_F/16$ Ma--Bertschinger conversion, r2126); **the projector was not.***
**★★ THE EFFECT, and it has the predicted $k$-dependence:**
| $k$ | 0.026 | 0.040 | 0.056 | 0.075 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| $\ell=175$ old | 0.930 | 0.916 | 0.867 | **0.813** |
| $\ell=175$ **new** | 0.942 | 0.928 | 0.906 | **0.875** |
| $\ell=450$ **new** | 0.951 | 0.935 | 0.916 | **0.883** |
| $\ell=750$ **new** | 1.000 | 0.940 | 0.897 | |
***$\sim\!6\%$ recovered at high $k$, growing with $k$*** — as an $O(k\tau_c)$ error must. **Derived from the
standard source form, not fitted.**
**⌗ THE TALLY SO FAR IN ITEM 1:** *(i) the $P_4$ "outlier" was **lensing** (r2161); (ii) `_visc` was
$16/45$ where the Silk formula derives $8/45$ — **a factor of 2**, worth half the deficit (r2163); (iii) the
$F_2$ handover seed **doubled $\sigma_\gamma$ across the seam**, worth $\sim\!1\%$ (r2163); (iv) the quadrupole
projector, worth $\sim\!6\%$ at high $k$ (r2166). ***All four were invisible against Planck's lensed spectrum
and only became pointable once the comparison was like-for-like and absolutely calibrated.***
**⌗ REMAINING: $\sim\!10$–$12\%$ at $k=0.075$, still to be characterised on the corrected machinery.**

comparable object and its **phase matches CAMB to 1 Mpc**; (ii) **$x_e$ is correct to $\sim\!1\%$** through the
visibility peak, retiring the r2121 FWHM worry; (iii) the mismatch is **real, in the early lobe
($\eta\approx255$–$265$), and now known NOT to be recombination, NOT acoustic phase, and NOT the handover.***
**⌗ STILL OPEN, and the candidate list is short:** *the $(1/k^{2})(g\theta_b)'$ Doppler term — **sharp exactly
where $g$ rises steeply**, which is that window — and the quadrupole contribution, omitted from both sides of
this particular comparison.*

## ★★★★★ BP · THE QUADRUPOLE KERNEL WAS HALVED — found by enumerating the source, not by testing values — r2187
**⌗ THE MOVE THAT FOUND IT.** *Every test of the previous ten revisions compared quantities **we and CAMB both
compute**. None could detect a term we lack entirely. ***So enumerate the standard source term by term against
ours.***
**★★★ AND TWO TERMS COMBINE.** *The SZ source carries both $[2]\;g\Pi/4$ and $[5]\;(3/4k^{2})(g\Pi)''$.
Integrating $[5]$ by parts twice, with $\dd^{2}j_\ell/\dd\eta^{2}=k^{2}j_\ell''$, gives $(3/4)(g\Pi)j_\ell''$
— so the **total** $\Pi$ contribution is*
$$\frac{g\Pi}{4}\bigl(j_\ell+3j_\ell''\bigr),\qquad\text{not}\qquad \frac{g\Pi}{4}\cdot\frac{j_\ell+3j_\ell''}{2}.$$
***r2166 added the kernel but carried HALF of it*** — right structure, half the amplitude, because I took the
$(3j_\ell''+j_\ell)/2$ form without checking which normalisation it belonged to.*
**★★ THE EFFECT:**
| $k$ | 0.014 | 0.026 | 0.040 | 0.056 | 0.075 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| halved | 0.993 | 0.935 | 0.924 | 0.902 | **0.873** |
| **restored** | 0.991 | **0.948** | **0.936** | **0.927** | **0.910** |
***$3$–$4\%$ recovered at high $k$, and the SLOPE largely gone*** *($0.948\to0.910$ against $0.935\to0.873$).*
**⌗ AND THE REMAINDER CHANGES CHARACTER.** *What is left is a $\sim\!7$–$9\%$ roughly **flat** offset above
$k\approx0.02$ — ***a step, not a slope***, which is a different class of cause from everything chased so far.*
**⌗ THE LESSON, and it is the fifth defect found this way:** ***a test that compares two things can only find
errors in things both have.*** *Enumeration against the standard form is a different instrument, and it found
in one pass what ten revisions of comparison could not.*

## ⛔ BU · THE STEP-SIZE HYPOTHESIS IS FALSIFIED — the freeze criterion's mechanism is NOT understood — r2193
**⌗ THE HYPOTHESIS.** *r2192 concluded the freeze cap was compensating for the **coarse stage's large steps** —
an explicit-RK4 limit $h\lambda\lesssim2.8$ with $\lambda\sim k$ — because a **single-stage** run was stable to
$k\eta/LG=2.85$ for every mode to $k=0.19$, while the **two-stage** run blew up without the cap.*
**⛔ IT IS WRONG.** *Replacing the criterion with $h\,k<\texttt{HKCAP}$ blows up at **2.0, 1.0, 0.5 and 0.35
alike**, where $k\eta<0.4\,LG$ is stable. **And $h\,k$ in the fine stage is $\sim\!0.06$ — far below any of
those** — so the failure is not in the coarse stage at all, and the two-stage/single-stage difference must be
something else (the fine stage there was $h=0.37$ against $0.52$ here).*
**✔ DEFAULT RESTORED, AND VERIFIED.** *I had changed `KETACAP`'s default to $9.9$ while testing — **which would
have shipped a broken production setting**. Restored to $0.4$ and the production comparison re-run to confirm
it is byte-for-byte the previous behaviour ($0.941/0.931/0.904$ at $\ell=150$). **`HKCAP` is left in place but
inert.**
**⌗ WHAT STANDS AND WHAT DOES NOT.** *Stands: the freeze **does** truncate the high-$k$ cancelling tails
(r2192, with the $\eta$-decomposition behind it), and a single-stage run **is** stable to $k\eta/LG=2.85$.
***Does not stand: any account of WHY the cap is needed.*** **The criterion is empirical and its mechanism is
unknown** — and that is now written where the knob is.*
**⚑ NEXT, and it must come before any fix:** *find what actually differs between the stable single-stage run
and the unstable two-stage one — **fine-stage step size ($0.37$ vs $0.52$), mode count, or the $k$-grid** —
by varying one at a time. ***No cap should be changed until that is known.***

## ★★★★★★ CC · THE ENVELOPE MEASURE: the residual is ONE smooth suppression, and every sharp feature was BINNING — r2201
**⌗ WHY A SECOND MEASURE.** *Every quantitative claim of this stretch came from **band powers** — $\int|\Delta_\ell|^2$
over a $k$-window, divided. If the two codes' $k$-sampling differs **within** a band, that appears as physics.
**The envelope method compares $|\Delta_\ell(k)|$ at its EXTREMA** — like at like, no window, and
well-conditioned (no zero crossings). *r2199 had already caught one binning artefact this way.*
**⛔ AND THE SHARP FEATURES DO NOT SURVIVE.** *Across $k=0.026$–$0.032$, where the bands showed
$0.966\to0.943$, the envelope ratios scatter **$0.98$–$1.02$ with no dip**. ***The "step" (r2167), the
"threshold at $k\approx0.02$" (r2165), the "$k$-oscillation" (r2199) and the "dip at $k\approx0.035$" (r2200)
are ALL the same binning artefact.*** **And with them goes r2168's "it moves with $\omega_b$" — there was no

**★★★ THE PROBLEM.** *Hoyle and Eddington: the Einstein--de~Sitter big bang **posits** the expansion it
explains — ***"only ever expanding because it was already expanding"*** — with **no account of the initial
rate**. **The objection was abandoned, not answered**; those who kept pressing it drifted toward crank status.
*A problem dropped is not a problem solved.*
**★★★ THE DISSOLUTION, read straight off `fig:F-triptych`.** *Panel (b): $\dd r/\dd s$ **zero at the
turnaround**, **unity at the Euclidean null**, **divergent at the branch point**. Panel (c): acceleration
**unbounded** across the same interval. ***The lift is the continuous physical process carrying the areal rate
from zero to infinity — over NO COSMIC TIME.*** **The universe arrives at $r=0$ with exactly Friedmann's
initial data**: vanishing radius, divergent rate, divergent deceleration — *the last working against a rate
already large enough to absorb it.* ***The big bang is not a point but a well-defined interval: a forced phase
shift of finite path length and zero duration.***
**★ ONTOLOGY-COST: costs the layered reading, and pays.** *The divergences are **effective** — perspectival
consequences of continuous parametric motion in $r$, **not dynamical causes**. ***The Shadow-Reading move, the
recurrence the census exists to exhibit, applied to the beginning itself.***
**⌗ AND HOW IT AROSE, which is the lesson.** ***The framework interpreted its own result.*** *The triptych was
already drawn and already correct; what was missing was reading it against $\operatorname{Re}\tilde\tau$ frozen.
**One clue-map site, worked properly, yielded a dissolution of a named standard problem** — on the *first*
entry of eighty-nine.

## ★★★★★★★★ CR · EDDINGTON NAMED THE MECHANISM IN 1933 — the historical record assembled at source — r2216
**★★★★★ THE FIND, and it is larger than the quote we went looking for.** *`thesischap1.tex` carries the
Eddington 1933 passage in full — **with both footnotes**. The one we sought is footnote 2 on Einstein--de
Sitter: *"**They do not state this in words, but it is the meaning of their mathematical formulae.**"* — the
charge being that ***the initial rate is not argued for but carried silently in the mathematics***.*
**★★★★★ BUT FOOTNOTE 1 IS THE ASTONISHING ONE:**
> *"For completeness we must add the possible hypothesis that **the system once extended much further than now,
> that it collapsed, and is now on the rebound**&hellip; the inward velocities being turned into outward velocities
> **by passage through the centre**. **So far as I know, this is not advocated by anyone.**"*
***Eddington describes this programme's mechanism in 1933 — collapse, and inward velocities turned outward by
passage through the centre — and sets it aside for want of an advocate.*** *His stated objection was the
**velocity distribution**; that is precisely what a Boltzmann transfer answers, and he had no means to compute
one. **What he could not see is that the passage is not a point but an INTERVAL.**
**⌗ AND THE MAIN INDICTMENT, verbatim:** *"These necessarily adopt the second alternative, and postulate that
the large velocities have existed from the beginning. ***This might be true; but can scarcely be called an
explanation of the large velocities.***" &hellip; *"leaves me cold. One cannot deny the possibility, but it is
difficult to see what **mental satisfaction** such a theory is supposed to afford."*
**⌗ CHANDRASEKHAR 1983 — the misreading, in the memorial.** *He inserts *"The curvature **[constant $\Lambda$]**
is, however, essentially determinable"* to argue Einstein--de Sitter had not rejected $\Lambda$. **The thesis,
at source: "it is absolutely clear&hellip; that the curvature of which they speak is not $\Lambda$, but the curvature
of three-dimensional space, $K$; and it is shocking that Chandrasekhar did not understand this."** *A misreading
inserted **while memorialising Eddington**, in service of a claim about the very paper Eddington had objected to.*
**⇒ HOW IT WAS LOST:** *Eddington, Hoyle and Bondi were all associated with $\Lambda$-advocacy or Steady State,
both empirically disfavoured. ***The objection was retired with its advocates*** — dismissed by association, not
answered. **The CMB settles THAT there was a hot dense beginning, not WHY it was expanding.** *The problem
remained as bad as ever; **it was not solved, it was dropped.***
**⌗ FILED: `HISTORICAL_CONTEXT_the_initial_rate_problem.md`** *— assembled **at source from the thesis**, not
from memory, and folded into census ENTRY 18.*

arbitrary datum.***

settled the associated disputes, establishes THAT there was a hot dense beginning, not WHY it was expanding.**

radius, named from this same book; and now **Eddington's own footnote foreshadowing the model** — with entry 18
