are ***the same expression, term for term***. **The classical selection rule IS the Euclidean kernel read
classically.**
**★★★★ AND IT SUPPLIES WHAT THE CLASSICAL READING COULD NOT.** *A Euclidean kernel run for a long imaginary

routes.**

below**. ***For the gravitational sector it is not***: the conformal factor enters with the **opposite sign**,
so the Euclidean gravitational action is **unbounded below** and $K$ **need not converge there at all**. *The
conformal-factor problem, arrived at by a route that makes it **unavoidable** rather than imported.*
$$\textbf{Secure for the matter content carried across; OPEN for the geometry carrying it.}$$

sector inherits is not an arbitrary datum but a prepared one*** — **constraining the matter initial condition
from above rather than leaving it to be fitted** — and **family 3's owed value acquires a candidate source**.

$$\textbf{That is the SAME condition the kernel enforces: regularity in the Euclidean continuation.}$$
***The extension-fixing condition and the lift's kernel are ONE REQUIREMENT MET TWICE*** — *once at the horizon,
once across the beginning. **The state the kernel projects onto is not a new posit; it is the one P10 had

along the segment), and the two sides' $\{q^A,p_A\}$ are related **through the kernel's action on modes**, not
by **a variational principle for the segment**. ***The gravitational sector is no longer the obstruction; that

## ★★★★★★★★★ DO · FAMILY 10 CLOSED — the adiabatic correction computed and LOCATED — r2239
**★ THE LAST ITEM.** *The kernel projects exactly only if $\hat H$ is constant, and it is not: the tensor tower
carries $\omega_n=\mu_n/a$ with $a$ varying along the lift, ***diverging in $\omega$ at the branch point***. So
the exact exponent is $\int\omega_n\dd s$ — **and it converges**, because $\lvert r\rvert\propto s^{2/3}$ makes
the integrand only $s^{-2/3}$:*
$$\int_0^{\pi\alpha/3}\frac{\dd s}{\lvert r\rvert}=3.3387\,\alpha^{-1}.$$
**⌗ AND IT RUNS THE CONSERVATIVE WAY.** *The constant-frequency estimate is $1.4396\,\alpha^{-1}$, so the exact
exponent is ***larger by $2.32$*** — **the suppression is STRONGER than the naive reading**, not weaker. *A
correction in the other direction would have threatened the selection rule; **this one reinforces it**.*

and a paper that says what was wanted invites exactly that reading.*** *The honesty belongs in **reporting the
adverse result at all** — which the passage does — **not in narrating the author's relation to it**. *The build
ledger is the place for how a result was arrived at; the paper is the place for what it is.**

## ★★★★★★ EB · THE CORRESPONDENCE STATED — P13 now says what a generation CONTAINS — r2251
**★ THE STANDARD MODEL SIDE, written out.** *One generation in left-handed Weyl form is five irreducible
multiplets of $\su(3)_c\times\su(2)_L\times\mathrm{u}(1)_Y$:*
$$Q:(\mathbf{3},\mathbf{2})_{+1/6},\quad u^c:(\bar{\mathbf{3}},\mathbf{1})_{-2/3},\quad d^c:(\bar{\mathbf{3}},\mathbf{1})_{+1/3},\quad L:(\mathbf{1},\mathbf{2})_{-1/2},\quad e^c:(\mathbf{1},\mathbf{1})_{+1}$$
*$6+3+3+2+1=\mathbf{15}$ Weyl fermions (16 with $\nu^c$); ***the quark/lepton distinction is exactly which
carries the colour $\mathbf{3}$***; three generations is $45$ (or $48$) in all.*
**✔ WHAT THE SECTOR FIXES — three things:** *the **number** of families (a $\gamma^5$-graded index,
$\dim\ker_+=3$); their **chirality** (*"not decoration: a construction returning a vector-like spectrum would
be excluded by that fact alone"*); and the **global $S_3$** relating them.*
**⛔ WHAT IT DOES NOT FIX — and the list is longer:** *the multiplet count ($15$ or $16$), the $\su(3)_c$
representations — ***hence which are quarks and which are leptons*** — the $\su(2)_L$ structure, the
hypercharges, and **anomaly cancellation**, which the fifteen satisfy non-trivially and which is among the
sharpest constraints on any proposed content. **Nor the masses, mixing, or $\nu^c$.**
$$\textbf{The zero-mode is ONE Weyl mode per wall, not fifteen: the index counts FAMILIES, not the states within them.}$$
**★★ AND TWO CONSEQUENCES DRAWN.** *(i) The correspondence is $3\leftrightarrow3$ **at the level of families**,
with every representation-theoretic fact on the far side of the wall — *compatible in the weak sense of not
contradicting, and **not** predictive*. (ii) ***The global $S_3$ is a point of contact, not a free addition***:
the Standard Model has **no exact family symmetry** (Yukawas break it badly), so an exact $S_3$ **must be
broken by whatever supplies the masses** — ***a constraint the sector inherits, recorded as an obligation and
not a result***.*
**✔ AND THE ABSTRACT CARRIES IT**, *because it is what a reader needs to locate the claim at all.*

## ★★★★★ EC · THE j-TOWER WORKED — a clean negative, and it closes the question — r2252
**⌗ THE QUESTION.** *P14's superpotential is $W=\lambda\sqrt f/r$ with $\lambda=j+\tfrac12$, and
Prop.~`wall` says each wall binds *"exactly one normalizable chiral zero-mode"*. ***Since $\lambda$ enters $W$,
does the wall problem supply multiplicity?*** *That was the one route on the list that could have produced
internal content **without crossing the gauge wall** — the obstruction being a theorem about **connected-group
isometry actions**, which a wall-localized index is not.*
**★ AND IT INTEGRATES IN CLOSED FORM.** *In the leaf frame's proper coordinate $\dd x=\dd r/\sqrt f$:*
$$\int W\,\dd x=\int\frac{\lambda\sqrt f}{r}\frac{\dd r}{\sqrt f}=\lambda\ln\lvert r\rvert
\quad\Longrightarrow\quad \psi=\lvert r\rvert^{\pm\lambda}.$$
***A power law, not the $\tanh$ model's exponential.***
**⌗ AND NORMALIZABILITY IS DECIDED BY THE BRANCH POINT.** *There $f\to-2M/r$, so
$\dd\ell\sim\sqrt{\lvert r\rvert/2M}\,\dd r$ and $\lvert r\rvert^{s}$ needs $s>-\tfrac34$. **The decaying branch
$s=+\lambda$ qualifies for every $\lambda$; the growing branch needs $\lambda<\tfrac34$, which no
$j+\tfrac12$ attains.** At the horizons the measure is an integrable inverse square root and the norm converges
(computed: $2.239$, $0.230$, $9.44\times10^{-5}$ at $\lambda=1,3,10$).*
$$\textbf{Exactly one bound mode per } (\text{wall},\,j)\textbf{ — the tower over } j \textbf{ is INFINITE.}$$
**⇒ THE NEGATIVE, and it is clean.** ***That is the ordinary angular decomposition of a four-dimensional
field, not multiplet content***: $j$ labels the partial wave of each generation's mode, and the index of
`sec:count` **counts walls, not partial waves**. *The internal content of a generation stays exactly where the
correspondence statement places it.*
**⌗ BAKED INTO P13 at the wall proposition**, *because ***the appearance of $\lambda$ in $W$ invites the
opposite reading*** and the paper should foreclose it rather than leave it for a reader to try.*

## ★★★★★★★ EI · THE C-FACE CHECKED IN THE ACTION — no branch-point-made asymmetry, verified — r2258
**⌗ THE IDENTIFICATION P16 CARRIES.** *The imaginary continuation reaching the conjugate branch, composed with
the standing $R$-conjugation, is ***the geometric factor of charge conjugation***:
$C=(Q\mapsto-Q)_{\rm field}\circ(R\circ K)_{\rm geometric}$, with the $r=0$ crossing **the seat of $C$'s
kinematic (Feynman--St\"uckelberg) face**. *That imaginary continuation is the lift.**
**★ SO THE CORPUS'S CLAIM BECOMES TESTABLE.** *It states matter and its antimatter progenitor are *"the two
ends of one standing $R$-conjugation and **no branch-point-made asymmetry**."* ***Tonight the lift acquired an
action*** — so the claim can be checked rather than argued from structure.*
**⛔ MY FIRST TEST USED THE WRONG CONJUGATION.** *I flipped $r$ alone and got $-0.0481$ against $-0.1443$ —
**not** equal and opposite, with the integrands differing by a constant $-4M$. ***P14 states $R$'s action
explicitly: the offset--mass relation is ODD, $2M\mapsto-2M$.*** *The mass must flip with the offset.*
**✔ WITH THE FULL $R$ IT IS EXACT.** *The integrand $r[f(r)-1]=-2M-r^{3}/\alpha^{2}$ is **odd pointwise** under
$(r,M)\mapsto(-r,-M)$:*
| $r$ | $-2M-r^3$ | $R$-image | sum |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.20 | $-0.39290$ | $+0.39290$ | $0$ |
| 0.45 | $-0.47603$ | $+0.47603$ | $0$ |
| 0.70 | $-0.72790$ | $+0.72790$ | $0$ |
$$S_E(\text{matter})=-0.1443\,\alpha^{2}/G,\qquad S_E(\text{antimatter})=+0.1443\,\alpha^{2}/G,\qquad \text{sum}=0.000\text{e}{+}00.$$
**⇒ NEITHER BRANCH IS WEIGHTED ABOVE THE OTHER BY THE CROSSING.** *The observed asymmetry is **not manufactured
there**, and any account of it must come from **the matter field's own charge-conjugation factor**, not from
the geometry carrying it. **The structural claim is now verified in the action.**
**⌗ AND THE FAILURE MODE IS INFORMATIVE:** *flipping $r$ alone leaves an even remainder $-4M$ — ***the
offset--mass oddness is doing the work here as well as in the chirality parity it fixes elsewhere***.

throat circle**, which is why the walls lie there and not elsewhere (p0, P14 `sec:chirality`), so **the wall

interval**.*

carries"*. **Its first application converts a conjecture-dependent claim in another paper into a structural
one.**

degeneracy and is **not** a branch point; $r=0$ is a branch point and has **no** Killing degeneracy.*

## ★★★★★★★ FO · THE WELL-POSED PROBLEM WORKED — the discrete route closed by computation — r2292
**⌗ THE HANDLE.** *P14's index **counts zero modes of an operator acting on a bundle** — one per wall per
internal state — so ***any multiplicity must come from the bundle's rank***, not from the walls. Two candidate
sources; both now excluded.*
**⛔ (1) NOT THE ANGULAR TOWER.** *$\lambda=j+\tfrac12$ labels **partial waves**, each contributing exactly one
bound mode (r2252).*
**⛔ (2) NOT THE DISCRETE RESIDUE — and this is the new one.** *$\mathrm{Aut}(A_2)=S_3\times\mathbb{Z}_2$, order
12, has irreps of dimensions $1,1,1,1,2,2$ (sum of squares $=12$, as required).*
$$\textbf{Its irreps top out at dimension TWO. The Standard Model needs } 6,3,3,2,1 .$$
*A reducible $\mathbf{1}\oplus\mathbf{2}$ supplies the **number** three, ***but a colour triplet is
irreducible*** — and irreducibility at that dimension is exactly what a group with two-dimensional irreps
cannot provide.*
**★ SO COLOUR IS CLOSED ON BOTH SIDES, FOR INDEPENDENT REASONS.** *The connected route by **the index
obstruction** (a theorem about circle actions); the discrete route by **the residue's representation theory**
(a finite computation).*
$$\textbf{The named opening is an opening for CHIRALITY, not for colour.}$$
*Which is what P13 said it was — ***now a computed statement rather than an inference from where the argument
stopped***.*
**⌗ AND THE HONEST GRADE:** *this is **not a step toward a derivation**. It is a statement that ***the two
places a reader would look first have been looked at***, and that a construction supplying representation
content must do so by means **neither the angular decomposition nor the discrete symmetry provides**. **Baked
in P13 and P14; no construction claimed.**

normalisation**:*

apply generation by generation**, and that separateness is CR's, not an assumption imported with the Standard
Model.

**⚑ AND AN EQUILATERAL TRIANGLE OF WEIGHTS PERMUTED BY CUBE ROOTS OF UNITY IS THE WEIGHT DIAGRAM OF THE
FUNDAMENTAL OF $\su(3)$**, *with the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ as its centre.*

## ★★★★ HW · CAMB'S SOURCE CONVENTION PINNED; THE BISECTION NOT YET CLEAN — r2354
**✔ `T_source` IS PINNED, self-validatingly.** *CAMB computes `delta_p_l_k` **independently** of the `T_source`
output, so projecting the latter and recovering the former is a genuine check:*
$$\Delta_\ell(k)=\int\dd\eta\, S(k,\eta)\, j_\ell\!\big(k(\eta_0-\eta)\big)$$
*reproduces CAMB's own $\Delta_\ell$ to **$0.7\%$ at $\ell=550$** and **$1.3\%$ at $\ell=815$** --- ***exactly
where the visibility dominates***, which is the regime the peaks live in. *(The $13\%$ at $\ell=200$ is my
$\eta$-grid truncating the ISW tail, not the convention.)* **Future source comparisons are now possible.**
**✔ AND AN ARGUMENT THAT NEEDS NO CONVENTION AT ALL.** *Converting the peak ratios to amplitudes: P2 is $0.906$,
P3 is $0.679$. Then $\ln(0.679)/\ln(0.906)=3.92$ across a $k^2$ ratio of $2.31$ ---*
$$\textbf{the excess scales as }k^{3.3}\textbf{, NOT }k^{2}.$$
***A mis-set diffusion scale gives exactly $k^2$*** *(an error $\epsilon$ in $k_D$ puts $\ln$(ratio)
$\propto(k/k_D)^2\,2\epsilon$), **and so does a too-wide visibility**. ***So the whole "the diffusion length is
off by $x\%$" class is excluded --- from our own numbers, independent of CAMB.***
**⛔ AND THE BISECTION IS NOT YET CLEAN --- stated rather than papered over.** *Two attempts, both
**phase-corrupted**: the sources oscillate in $\eta$ and $\Delta_\ell(k)$ oscillates in $k$, so **pointwise
comparison is phase-sensitive**. The first gave sign-alternating ratios; the second gave $1.47/1.74/1.68$ ---
***non-monotone, $\pm10\%$, and not growing with $\ell$***, so whatever it measures **is not an
$\ell$-dependent projection loss**, but it is not a clean null either.
**⇒ WHAT THE NEXT CONSTRUCTION MUST BE:** ***a $k$-band-integrated comparison, not pointwise*** --- the same
lesson as the envelope measure, which compares at extrema precisely because the raw quantities oscillate.
**A fourth ambiguous table would have been worse than stopping.**

## ★★★★★★★★★★ HX · THE DEFICIT IS DOWNSTREAM OF $\Delta_\ell$ — the band bisection — r2355
**★ THE DECISIVE MEASUREMENT.** *Band power $B_\ell=\int\dd k\,k^{n_s-2}|\Delta_\ell(k)|^2$ --- the quantity
that actually enters $C_\ell$, and **phase-insensitive by construction** since the oscillations are squared and
integrated over --- computed for **ours** and **CAMB** on the **same $k$-grid, the same $\eta$-grid, and with
the same projection code**:*
| $\ell$ | band ratio (normalised to $\ell=221$) | peak deficit to explain |
|---|---|---|
| $221$ | $1.000$ | $1.000$ |
| $537$ | ***$1.003$*** | $0.906$ |
| $815$ | ***$1.039$*** | $0.679$ |
$$\textbf{Our }\Delta_\ell(k)\textbf{ agrees with CAMB's to }0.3\%\textbf{ at }\ell=537\textbf{ and }3.9\%\textbf{ at }\ell=815.$$
***The source and the projection are both sound. The $10.4\%$/$47.3\%$ peak excess is DOWNSTREAM of
$\Delta_\ell$*** --- in how $C_\ell$ is assembled from it. **One measurement excludes the damping physics, the
tight-coupling treatment, the hierarchy, the visibility and the projection kernel**, after four earlier attempts
that were phase-corrupted and uninterpretable.
**⌗ AND TWO CONTROLS DID THEIR JOB EN ROUTE.** *(1) Fitting the per-mode normalisation from the **RMS of
oscillating sources** gave **$47\%$ scatter** and an $\ell=221$ control of **$1.605$** --- ***correctly
unreadable***, and it exposed that I was comparing our **monopole+ISW** against CAMB's **full** source. (2) The
fix: **each term through its own kernel** (Doppler with $j_\ell'$, quadrupole with $j_\ell+3j_\ell''$), and the
normalisation taken **analytically** --- $\sqrt{A_s}(k/k_{\rm piv})^{(n_s-1)/2}$, ***known, not fitted*** --- at
which point the $\ell$-dependence came out clean. **An overall constant remains and is irrelevant to the
$\ell$-dependence.**
**⌗ THE LIVE CANDIDATE, mid-test.** *The spectrum samples `ls = arange(LMIN, lmax, DL)` with **`DL=15`** and
extracts peaks by **three-point parabolic refinement**. ***Sampling every 15 in $\ell$ and fitting a parabola
under-resolves narrower peaks, and peaks narrow with $\ell$*** --- a deficit **growing with $\ell$**, which is
the observed signature. `DL=15` reproduces the baseline exactly ($218.9/527.5/793.7$, $P_1/P_2=2.431$); the
**`DL=5` comparison was still running** when the session's tool budget ran out. ***Needs re-running.***

## ★★★★★★★★★★★★★ ID · THE VISCOSITY COEFFICIENT DERIVED — a factor 2 the file contradicted itself on — r2361

**⌗ THE COMPLAINT WAS FAIR.** *I set the $10\%$ down and left the sub-percent number standing as "wrong"
**without asking why it came out sub-percent**. It has a reason, and the reason is worth more than either
number alone.
**✔ THE TWO COMPUTATIONS DIFFER IN **TWO** THINGS AT ONCE --- the RATE and the LOWER LIMIT. Varied one at a
time:**
| configuration | $r_D$ | vs $\Lambda$CDM |
|---|---|---|
| radiation **IN**, from zero *(= $\Lambda$CDM)* | $3.4754$ | $+0.0\%$ |
| radiation **IN**, cut at onset *(**LIMIT** only)* | $3.4622$ | ***$-0.4\%$*** |
| radiation **OUT**, from zero *(**RATE** only)* | $3.8481$ | ***$+10.7\%$*** |
| radiation **OUT**, cut at onset *(**BOTH** $=$ CR)* | $3.8244$ | $+10.0\%$ |
**⇒ SO THE $0.4\%$ COMPUTATION HELD THE RATE FIXED AND VARIED ONLY THE LIMIT.** *It gives sub-percent because
***the onset truncation genuinely IS a sub-percent effect*** --- a real and useful fact --- but it had already
assumed CR's rate was $\Lambda$CDM's, so **it never asked the question the $10\%$ answers**.
**★ AND THE DECOMPOSITION IS WORTH HAVING ON ITS OWN:** *the signature is **entirely the radiation term in the
rate** ($+10.7\%$ alone), with the onset cutoff ***reducing*** it slightly ($-0.4\%$) to the net $+10.0\%$.
***Two effects, opposite signs, twenty-five times apart*** --- and **neither was visible while they were
bundled**.
**⇒ WHICH SHARPENS THE PHYSICS TO ONE SENTENCE:** ***does the plasma's diffusion run on a rate that carries
radiation's gravity, or not?*** *Everything else is a fraction of a per cent. **The stacking rate is the
geometry --- radiation is not in the kernel, it needs a bend --- but the plasma is IN the content, and the
content is what bends the leaf.*** $10\%$ **is exactly the size of that ambiguity, and it is the whole of the
damping signature.**

## ✔ IM · THE $\Psi$ ANISOTROPIC-STRESS SIGN CORRECTED IN BOTH CR RECEIPTS — r2371
**⌗ THE ERROR, and it is unambiguous.** *The standard relation is $k^2(\Phi-\Psi)=12\pi Ga^2(\rho+p)\sigma$,
i.e. ***$\Psi=\Phi-(\text{positive})\sigma$***. Both CR receipts carried a **PLUS** at both sites --- inside the
RHS and again when $\Psi$ is formed at recombination.
**✔ AND IT IS NOT A COMPENSATING CONVENTION, checked rather than assumed:** *the neutrino hierarchy is
***byte-identical*** in the CR receipts and in HIER --- `out[:,7]=(8/15)*tn-(3/5)*kk*F[:,1]` --- so $\sigma$
carries the same sign in each; and **HIER pairs that same $\sigma$ with a MINUS while reproducing CAMB's peak
ratios to $0.42\%$**. *The initial $\Phi$ is negative in both. **Same conventions throughout, opposite sign on
one term.***
**★ WHAT IT IS WORTH, measured before committing:**
| `ROBUST_p1p2_scan` | peaks | $P_1/P_2$ | $P_1/P_3$ |
|---|---|---|---|
| as written ($+$) | $150,345,555,795$ | $1.121$ | $1.300$ |
| **corrected ($-$)** | $150,\mathbf{360},555,795$ | ***$1.347$*** | ***$1.463$*** |
| sky | $220,536,813$ | $2.212$ | $2.257$ |
***$+20\%$ on $P_1/P_2$ and $+13\%$ on $P_1/P_3$, both toward the sky.***
**✔ AND THE CITED CLAIM SURVIVES:** *`C11TEST` is cited for *"the potential does decay on the observable leg,
**by a factor of order two** across the first few peaks"*. Corrected: $\Phi(\rm rec)/\Phi(\rm seam)$ goes
$0.603\to0.592$ at $\ell=228$ --- **a factor $1.66\to1.69$, still of order two**. ***The correction does not
disturb what the receipt is cited for.***
**⌗ AND WHAT IT DOES NOT FIX, stated:** ***the first peak stays at $150$*** either way. **The anisotropic stress
is not what places it** --- the velocities are zero at $\eta_{\rm onset}$, so the first extremum sits at a fixed
phase from the start and where it lands is set by the span $\eta_{\rm rec}-\eta_{\rm onset}=269$ Mpc. *That is
the corpus's stated disagreement, and the $z_{\rm onset}$ scan showed it cannot be tuned away because $\ell_A$
and $\ell_1$ move together.*

## ★★★★★★ IN · THE CR ARM GETS DAMPING — the Thomson opacity ported — r2372
**⌗ THE GAP.** *The CR receipts evolved photons as a **perfect fluid**: no $\sigma_\gamma$, ***no Silk damping at
all***. HIER's full hierarchy is a large port --- opacity, recombination, the E-mode tower --- but ***the
tight-coupling closure $\sigma_\gamma=A\theta/\tau'$ needs only $\tau'$***.
**✔ AND $\tau'$ TRANSFERS DIRECTLY, which is what made this bounded.** *$\tau'=n_e\sigma_T a$ is **a function of
the scale factor alone** --- it does not know which background it is read on --- so HIER's recombination history
(`RD_diffusion_direct`'s Peebles solve) applies unchanged on CR's rate. **Ported, with $A=16/45$ and the slip
term, both as derived at r2361.**
**⛔ AND THE FIRST RUN NaN'd, which was the equations talking.** *The closure is a **tight-coupling** result and
diverges as $\tau'\to0$; the CR receipt runs one system past recombination. ***Gated at $\tau'=0.6$, HIER's own
switch threshold*** --- **after decoupling the photons free-stream and a fluid viscosity has no meaning**, so
zeroing it there is the physical statement, not a patch.

|---|---|---|
| as found | $1.121$ | $1.300$ |

**⌗ THE QUADRUPOLE ADDITION IS A NULL, AND CORRECTLY SO:** *$\sigma_\gamma=A\theta/\tau'$ is **suppressed by
$1/\tau'$**, so during tight coupling the photon quadrupole is negligible --- ***which is what the
tight-coupling limit means***. It matters near and after decoupling, **where the gate turns it off because
there is no hierarchy to compute it with**. *HIER gets that contribution from the hierarchy after its switch.*
**⇒ SO THE REMAINING GAP IS THE HIERARCHY ITSELF** *and the first-peak position. **Three corrections have closed
about a quarter of the distance to the sky**; the rest is the corpus's named frontier.*

## ★★★★★★★★★★★ IP · THE TWO CR IMPLEMENTATIONS AGREE — the disagreement was a peak-finder artefact — r2376
**⌗ THE APPARENT CONFLICT.** *HIER-CR reported peaks $331.9/506.8/727.7/982.0$ with $P_1/P_2=0.749$ --- **the
second peak stronger than the first** --- against the receipt's $150/360/555/795$ with $1.409$. **A factor of
two, and a qualitatively different spectrum.**
**★ AND IT WAS THE FINDER.** *A band-power scan of the **same run** puts maxima at*
$$\ell \simeq 140,\ 320,\ 500,\ 700 \qquad \text{(troughs at } 240,\ 420,\ 620\text{)}$$
***HIER's peak-finder missed the first one***: `LMIN=120` with `DL=30` leaves too few samples below
$\ell\simeq140$ for `argrelextrema(order=2)` to register a maximum, **so every label was shifted by one and
its "$P_1/P_2$" was really $P_2/P_3$**.
**✔ CORRECTLY IDENTIFIED, THE TWO AGREE:**
$$\text{HIER-CR: } P_1(140)/P_2(320)=1.54 \qquad\text{receipt: } 1.409$$
*Within about $10\%$ --- **which the receipt's missing baryon gravity and instantaneous recombination would
plausibly account for**.
**✔ SO THE CORPUS'S CITED CLAIM IS CONFIRMED, not threatened.** *"A perturbation calculation built on the branch
point handover places it near $\ell=150$" ***now holds in a full photon-hierarchy calculation*** --- with
polarisation, damping, baryon gravity, a proper visibility function and line-of-sight integration --- **on an
instrument reproducing $\Lambda$CDM's peaks to $0.1\%$ and its ratios to $0.27\%$ against CAMB.**
**⇒ AND THE DISAGREEMENT WITH THE SKY IS REAL AND ROBUST:** *$\ell_1\simeq140$--$150$ against $220$. **Every
implementation agrees on it**, and the $z_{\rm onset}$ scan showed it cannot be tuned away because $\ell_A$ and
$\ell_1$ move together.*
**✔ TWO FIXES:** *`LMIN` is now **arm-aware** ($40$ for CR, $120$ for $\Lambda$CDM); and the finder **detects
edge maxima explicitly** and says so, rather than dropping them silently.*
**⌗ AND TWO DEFECTS IN THE RECEIPTS, QUANTIFIED ON THE WAY:** *they carry **no baryon term in the constraint**
--- their state has no $\delta_b$, so baryons load the photons through $R$ but ***never gravitate***, $14\%$ of
the matter missing from the source --- and **instantaneous recombination**, measured on the $\Lambda$CDM arm as
***$-20\%$ on $P_1/P_2$ and $-28\%$ on $P_1/P_3$***, with peaks shifted **up** $5\%$.
